element. The element content is defined in the WS-Trust namespace. This
specification does not further define or limit the content of this element or
the wst:Claims/@Dialect attribute as it is out of scope of this document. </span></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;text-autospace:none'><span
style='color:black'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;text-autospace:none'><span
style='color:black'>This element indicates the REQUIRED claims that the
security token MUST contain in order to satisfy the requirements of the token
assertion. </span></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;text-autospace:none'><span
style='color:black'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;text-autospace:none'><span
style='color:black'>Individual token assertions MAY further limit what claims MAY
be specified for that specific token assertion. </span></p>

<h3><a name="_Toc325572726"></a><a name="_Toc182291505"></a><a
name="_Toc161826529">5.2.4 Processing Rules and Token Matching</a> </h3>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;text-autospace:none'><span
style='color:black'>The sender is free to compose the requirements expressed by
token assertions inside the receiver’s policy to as many tokens as it sees fit.
As long as the union of all tokens in the received message contains the REQUIRED
set of claims from REQUIRED token issuers the message is valid according to the
receiver’s policy. </span></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;text-autospace:none'><span
style='color:black'>For example if the receiver’s policy contains two token
assertions, one requires IssuedToken from issuer A with claims C1 and C2 and
the second requires IssuedToken from issuer B with claims C3 and C4, the sender
can satisfy such requirements with any of the following security token
decomposition: </span></p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;text-autospace:none'><span
style='color:black'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<ol style='margin-top:0in' start=1 type=1>
 <li class=MsoNormal style='color:black;margin-top:0in;margin-bottom:0in;
     margin-bottom:.0001pt;text-autospace:none'>Two tokens, T1 and T2. T1 is
     issued by issuer A and contains claims C1 and C2 and T2 is issued by
     issuer B and contains claims C3 and C4. </li>
 <li class=MsoNormal style='color:black;margin-top:0in;margin-bottom:0in;
     margin-bottom:.0001pt;text-autospace:none'>Three tokens, T1, T2 and T3. T1
     is issued by issuer A and contains claim C1, T2 is also issued by issuer A
     and contains claim C2 and T3 is issued by issuer B and contains claims C3
     and C4. </li>
 <li class=MsoNormal style='color:black;margin-top:0in;margin-bottom:0in;
     margin-bottom:.0001pt;text-autospace:none'>Three tokens, T1, T2 and T3. T1
     is issued by issuer A and contains claims C1 and C2, T2 is issued by
     issuer B and contains claim C3 and T3 is also issued by issuer B and
     contains claim C4. </li>
 <li class=MsoNormal style='color:black;margin-top:0in;margin-bottom:0in;
     margin-bottom:.0001pt;text-autospace:none'>Four tokens, T1, T2, T3 and T4.
     T1 is issued by issuer A and contains claim C1, T2 is also issued by
     issuer A and contains claim C2, T3 is issued by issuer B and contains
     claim C3 and T4 is also issued by issuer B and contains claim C4. </li>
</ol>

<h2><a name="_Toc99269242"></a><a name="_Toc325572727"></a><a
name="_Toc182291506"></a><a name="_Toc161826530"></a><a name="_Toc104103941">5.3
Token Properties</a></h2>

<h3><a name="_Toc325572728"></a><a name="_Toc182291507"></a><a
name="_Toc161826531"></a><a name="_Toc104103942">5.3.1 [Derived Keys] Property</a></h3>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
property specifies whether derived keys SHOULD be used as defined in
WS-SecureConversation. If the value is 'true', derived keys MUST be used. If
the value is 'false', derived keys MUST NOT be used. The value of this property
applies to a specific token. The value of this property is populated by
assertions specific to the token. The default value for this property is
'false'.</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>See the [Explicit
Derived Keys] and [Implied Derived Key] properties below for information on how
particular forms of derived keys are specified.</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Where the key
material associated with a token is asymmetric, this property applies to the
use of symmetric keys encrypted with the key material associated with the
token.</span></p>

<h3><a name="_Toc325572729"></a><a name="_Toc182291508"></a><a
name="_Toc161826532">5.3.2 [Explicit Derived Keys] Property</a></h3>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
property specifies whether Explicit Derived Keys (see Section 7 of [</span><a
href="#wssecureconversation"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WS-SecureConversation</span></a><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>]) are allowed. If the value is 'true'
then Explicit Derived Keys MAY be used. If the value is 'false' then Explicit
Derived Keys MUST NOT be used.</span></p>

<h3><a name="_Toc325572730"></a><a name="_Toc182291509"></a><a
name="_Toc161826533">5.3.3 [Implied Derived Keys] Property</a></h3>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
property specifies whether Implied Derived Keys (see Section 7.3 of
[WS-SecureConversation]) are allowed. If the value is 'true' then Implied Derived
Keys MAY be used. If the value is 'false' then Implied Derived Keys MUST NOT be
used.</span></p>

<h2><a name="_Toc325572731"></a><a name="_Toc182291510"></a><a
name="_Toc161826534"></a><a name="_Toc104103943"></a><a name="_Toc133985015"></a>5.4
Token Assertion Types</h2>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
sections describe the token assertions defined as part of this specification.</span></p>

<h3><a name="_Toc325572732"></a><a name="_Toc182291511"></a><a
name="_Toc161826535"></a><a name="_Toc104103944"></a><a name="_Toc99269243"></a><a
name="_UsernameToken_Assertion"></a>5.4.1 UsernameToken Assertion</h3>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This element
represents a requirement to include a username token.</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>There are cases
where encrypting the UsernameToken is reasonable. For example:</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>1.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>When transport
security is not used.</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>2.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>When a plaintext
password is used.</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>3.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>When a weak
password hash is used.</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>4.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>When the username
needs to be protected, e.g. for privacy reasons.</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>When the
UsernameToken is to be encrypted it SHOULD be listed as a
SignedEncryptedSupportingToken (Section 8.5), EndorsingEncryptedSupportingToken
(Section 8.6) or SignedEndorsingEncryptedSupportingToken (Section 8.7).</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:
solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in;
background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;sp:UsernameToken
sp:IncludeToken=&quot;<i>xs:anyURI</i>&quot;? xmlns:sp=&quot;...&quot; ... &gt;<br>
  ( </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    &lt;sp:Issuer&gt;wsa:EndpointReferenceType&lt;/sp:Issuer&gt;
|  </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    &lt;sp:IssuerName&gt;xs:anyURI&lt;/sp:IssuerName&gt;
</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  ) ?
</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  &lt;wst:Claims
Dialect=&quot;...&quot;&gt; ... &lt;/wst:Claims&gt; ?</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'> 
&lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp=&quot;...&quot;&gt;<br>
    (<br>
      &lt;sp:NoPassword ... /&gt; |<br>
      &lt;sp:HashPassword ... /&gt;<br>
    ) ?<br>
    (<br>
      &lt;sp:RequireDerivedKeys /&gt; |<br>
      &lt;sp:RequireImpliedDerivedKeys ... /&gt; |<br>
      &lt;sp:RequireExplicitDerivedKeys ... /&gt; <br>
    ) ?<br>
    (<br>
      &lt;sp:WssUsernameToken10 ... /&gt; |<br>
      &lt;sp:WssUsernameToken11 ... /&gt;<br>
    ) ?<br>
    ...<br>
  &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt; <br>
  ...<br>
&lt;/sp:UsernameToken&gt;</p>

</div>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined above:</span></p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'>UsernameToken</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This identifies a UsernameToken assertion.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:UsernameToken/@sp:IncludeToken</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL attribute identifies the token inclusion
value for this token assertion.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:UsernameToken/sp:Issuer</span>
</p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element, of type wsa:EndpointReferenceType,
contains reference to the issuer of the sp:UsernameToken. </p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:UsernameToken/sp:IssuerName
</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element, of type xs:anyURI, contains the
logical name of the sp:UsernameToken issuer. </p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:UsernameToken/wst:Claims
</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element identifies the REQUIRED claims that a
security token must contain in order to satisfy the token assertion
requirements.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:UsernameToken/wsp:Policy</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This REQUIRED element identifies additional requirements for
use of the sp:UsernameToken assertion.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:UsernameToken/wsp:Policy/sp:NoPassword</span></p>

<p class=DefinedTerm style='margin-left:.5in'><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates that the wsse:Password
element MUST NOT be present in the Username token.</span></p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:UsernameToken/wsp:Policy/sp:HashPassword</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in'><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates that the wsse:Password
element MUST be present in the Username token and that the content of the
wsse:Password element MUST contain a hash of the timestamp, nonce and password
as defined in [WSS: Username Token Profile].</span></p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:UsernameToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireDerivedKeys</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that sets the
[Derived Keys], [Explicit Derived Keys] and [Implied Derived Keys] properties
for this token to 'true'.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:UsernameToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireExplicitDerivedKeys</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in'><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that sets the [Derived Keys] and
[Explicit Derived Keys] properties for this token to 'true' and the [Implied Derived
Keys] property for this token to 'false'.</span></p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:UsernameToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireImpliedDerivedKeys</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in'><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that sets the [Derived Keys] and [Implied
Derived Keys] properties for this token to 'true' and the [Explicit Derived
Keys] property for this token to 'false'.</span></p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:UsernameToken/wsp:Policy/sp:WssUsernameToken10</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in'><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates that a Username token
should be used as defined in [</span><a href="#wssusername10"><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WSS:UsernameTokenProfile1.0</span></a><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>].</span></p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:UsernameToken/wsp:Policy/sp:WssUsernameToken11</span></p>

<p class=Definitionterm style='margin-top:4.0pt;margin-right:0in;margin-bottom:
4.0pt;margin-left:.5in'><span style='font-weight:normal'>This OPTIONAL element
is a policy assertion that indicates that a Username token should be used as
defined in [</span><a href="#wssusername11"><span style='font-weight:normal'>WSS:UsernameTokenProfile1.1</span></a><span
style='font-weight:normal'>].</span></p>

<h3><a name="_Toc325572733"></a><a name="_Toc182291512"></a><a
name="_Toc161826536"></a><a name="_Toc104103945"></a><a name="_Toc99269244"></a><a
name="_Toc96244142"></a><a name="_Toc95877088"></a><a name="_Toc95113102"></a><a
name="_Toc92518194"></a><a name="_IssuedToken_Assertion"></a>5.4.2 IssuedToken
Assertion</h3>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This element
represents a requirement for an issued token, which is one issued by some token
issuer using the mechanisms defined in WS-Trust. This assertion is used in 3<sup>rd</sup>
party scenarios. For example, the initiator may need to request a SAML token
from a given token issuer in order to secure messages sent to the recipient.</span></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:
solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in;
background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;sp:IssuedToken
sp:IncludeToken=&quot;<i>xs:anyURI</i>&quot;? xmlns:sp=&quot;...&quot; ... &gt;<br>
  (</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'> 
&lt;sp:Issuer&gt;<i>wsa:EndpointReferenceType</i>&lt;/sp:Issuer&gt; |</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  &lt;sp:IssuerName&gt;xs:anyURI&lt;/sp:IssuerName&gt;
</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  ) ?</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  &lt;wst:Claims
Dialect=&quot;...&quot;&gt; ... &lt;/wst:Claims&gt; ?<br>
  &lt;sp:RequestSecurityTokenTemplate TrustVersion=&quot;<i>xs:anyURI</i>&quot;?
&gt;<br>
    ...<br>
  &lt;/sp:RequestSecurityTokenTemplate&gt;<br>
  &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp=&quot;...&quot;&gt;<br>
    (<br>
      &lt;sp:RequireDerivedKeys ... /&gt; |<br>
      &lt;sp:RequireImpliedDerivedKeys ... /&gt; |<br>
      &lt;sp:RequireExplicitDerivedKeys ... /&gt; <br>
    ) ?<br>
    &lt;sp:RequireExternalReference ... /&gt; ?<br>
    &lt;sp:RequireInternalReference ... /&gt; ?<br>
    ...<br>
  &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt; <br>
  ...<br>
&lt;/sp:IssuedToken&gt;</p>

</div>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined above:</span></p>

<p class=MsoNormal>/sp:IssuedToken</p>

<p class=Definition>This identifies an IssuedToken assertion.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:IssuedToken/@sp:IncludeToken</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL attribute identifies the token inclusion
value for this token assertion.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal>/sp:IssuedToken/sp:Issuer</p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element, of type wsa:EndpointReferenceType,
contains a reference to the issuer for the issued token.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:IssuedToken/sp:IssuerName
</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element, of type xs:anyURI, contains the
logical name of the sp:IssuedToken issuer. </p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:IssuedToken/wst:Claims
</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element identifies the REQUIRED claims that a
security token must contain in order to satisfy the token assertion
requirements.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal>/sp:IssuedToken/sp:RequestSecurityTokenTemplate</p>

<p class=Definition>This REQUIRED element contains elements which MUST be
copied into the wst:SecondaryParameters of the RST request sent to the
specified issuer. Note: the initiator is NOT REQUIRED to understand the
contents of this element.</p>

<p class=Definition>See Appendix B for details of the content of this element.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal>/sp:IssuedToken/sp:RequestSecurityTokenTemplate/@TrustVersion</p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in'><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
OPTIONAL attribute contains a WS-Trust specification namespace URI identifying
the version of WS-Trust referenced by the contents of this element.</span></p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:IssuedToken/wsp:Policy</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This REQUIRED element identifies additional requirements
for use of the sp:IssuedToken assertion.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:IssuedToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireDerivedKeys</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in'><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that sets the [Derived Keys], [Explicit
Derived Keys] and [Implied Derived Keys]   properties for this token to 'true'.</span></p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:IssuedToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireExplicitDerivedKeys</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in'><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that sets the [Derived Keys] and
[Explicit Derived Keys] properties for this token to 'true' and the [Implied
Derived Keys] property for this token to 'false'.</span></p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:IssuedToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireImpliedDerivedKeys</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in'><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that sets the [Derived Keys] and [Implied
Derived Keys] properties for this token to 'true' and the [Explicit Derived
Keys] property for this token to 'false'.</span></p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:IssuedToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireInternalReference</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in'><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates whether an internal
reference is REQUIRED when referencing this token.<br>
Note: This reference will be supplied by the issuer of the token.</span></p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:IssuedToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireExternalReference</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in'><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates whether an external
reference is REQUIRED when referencing this token.<br>
Note: This reference will be supplied by the issuer of the token.</span></p>

<p class=MsoNormal>Note: The IssuedToken MAY or MAY NOT be associated with key
material and such key material may be symmetric or asymmetric. The Binding
assertion will imply the type of key associated with this token. Services MAY
also include information in the <span style='font-family:"Courier New"'>sp:RequestSecurityTokenTemplate</span>
element to explicitly define the expected key type. See <a href="#AppendixB">Appendix
B</a> for details of the <span class=CodeChar><span style='font-size:9.0pt'>sp:RequestSecurityTokenTemplate</span></span>
element.</p>

<h3><a name="_Toc325572734"></a><a name="_Toc182291513"></a><a
name="_Toc161826537"></a><a name="_Toc104103946"></a><a name="_Toc99269245"></a><a

name="_Toc92518195"></a><a name="_X509Token_Assertion"></a>5.4.3 X509Token
Assertion</h3>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This element
represents a requirement for a binary security token carrying an X509 token.</span></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:

background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;sp:X509Token
sp:IncludeToken=&quot;<i>xs:anyURI</i>&quot;? xmlns:sp=&quot;...&quot; ... &gt;<br>
  ( </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    &lt;sp:Issuer&gt;wsa:EndpointReferenceType&lt;/sp:Issuer&gt;
|  </p>

</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  ) ?
</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  &lt;wst:Claims
Dialect=&quot;...&quot;&gt; ... &lt;/wst:Claims&gt; ?</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'> 
&lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp=&quot;...&quot;&gt;<br>
    (<br>
      &lt;sp:RequireDerivedKeys ... /&gt; |<br>
      &lt;sp:RequireExplicitDerivedKeys ... /&gt; |<br>
      &lt;sp:RequireImpliedDerivedKeys ... /&gt;<br>
    ) ?<br>
    &lt;sp:RequireKeyIdentifierReference ... /&gt; ?<br>
    &lt;sp:RequireIssuerSerialReference ... /&gt; ?<br>
    &lt;sp:RequireEmbeddedTokenReference ... /&gt; ?<br>
    &lt;sp:RequireThumbprintReference ... /&gt; ?<br>
    (<br>
      &lt;sp:WssX509V3Token10 ... /&gt; |<br>
      &lt;sp:WssX509Pkcs7Token10 ... /&gt; |<br>
      &lt;sp:WssX509PkiPathV1Token10 ... /&gt; |<br>
      &lt;sp:WssX509V1Token11 ... /&gt; |<br>
      &lt;sp:WssX509V3Token11 ... /&gt; |<br>
      &lt;sp:WssX509Pkcs7Token11 ... /&gt; |<br>
      &lt;sp:WssX509PkiPathV1Token11 ... /&gt;<br>
    ) ?<br>
    ...<br>
  &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt; <br>
  ...<br>
&lt;/sp:X509Token&gt;</p>

</div>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined above:</span></p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'>X509Token</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This identifies an X509Token assertion.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/@sp:IncludeToken</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL attribute identifies the token inclusion
value for this token assertion.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/sp:Issuer</span>
</p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element, of type wsa:EndpointReferenceType,
contains reference to the issuer of the sp:X509Token. </p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/sp:IssuerName
</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element, of type xs:anyURI, contains the
logical name of the sp:X509Token issuer. </p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/wst:Claims
</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element identifies the REQUIRED claims that a
security token must contain in order to satisfy the token assertion
requirements.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/wsp:Policy</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This REQUIRED element identifies additional requirements
for use of the sp:X509Token assertion.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireDerivedKeys</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that sets the
[Derived Keys], [Explicit Derived Keys] and [Implied Derived Keys] properties
for this token to 'true'.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireExplicitDerivedKeys</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that sets the
[Derived Keys] and [Explicit Derived Keys] properties for this token to 'true'
and the [Implied Derived Keys] property for this token to 'false'.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireImpliedDerivedKeys</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that sets the
[Derived Keys] and [Implied Derived Keys] properties for this token to 'true'
and the [Explicit Derived Keys] property for this token to 'false'.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireKeyIdentifierReference</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that a key identifier reference is REQUIRED when referencing this token.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireIssuerSerialReference</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that an issuer serial reference is REQUIRED when referencing this token.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireEmbeddedTokenReference</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that an embedded token reference is REQUIRED when referencing this token.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireThumbprintReference</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that a thumbprint reference is REQUIRED when referencing this token.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/wsp:Policy/sp:WssX509V3Token10</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that an X509 Version 3 token should be used as defined in [<a href="#wssx50910">WSS:X509TokenProfile1.0</a>].</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/wsp:Policy/sp:WssX509Pkcs7Token10</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that an X509 PKCS7 token should be used as defined in [<a href="#wssx50910">WSS:X509TokenProfile1.0</a>].</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/wsp:Policy/sp:WssX509PkiPathV1Token10</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that an X509 PKI Path Version 1 token should be used as defined in [<a
href="#wssx50910">WSS:X509TokenProfile1.0</a>].</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/wsp:Policy/sp:WssX509V1Token11</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that an X509 Version 1 token should be used as defined in [<a href="#wssx50911">WSS:X509TokenProfile1.1</a>].</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/wsp:Policy/sp:WssX509V3Token11</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that an X509 Version 3 token should be used as defined in [<a href="#wssx50911">WSS:X509TokenProfile1.1</a>].</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/wsp:Policy/sp:WssX509Pkcs7Token11</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that an X509 PKCS7 token should be used as defined in [<a href="#wssx50911">WSS:X509TokenProfile1.1</a>].</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/wsp:Policy/sp:WssX509PkiPathV1Token11</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that an X509 PKI Path Version 1 token should be used as defined in [<a
href="#wssx50911">WSS:X509TokenProfile1.1</a>].</p>

<h3><a name="_Toc325572736"></a><a name="_Toc182291515"></a><a
name="_Toc161826539"></a><a name="_Toc104103948"></a><a
name="_SpnegoContextToken_Assertion"></a>5.4.5 SpnegoContextToken Assertion</h3>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This element
represents a requirement for a SecurityContextToken obtained by executing an
n-leg RST/RSTR SPNEGO binary negotiation protocol with the Web Service, as
defined in WS-Trust.</span></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:

background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;sp:SpnegoContextToken
sp:IncludeToken=&quot;<i>xs:anyURI</i>&quot;? xmlns:sp=&quot;...&quot; ... &gt;<br>
  (</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'> 
&lt;sp:Issuer&gt;<i>wsa:EndpointReferenceType</i>&lt;/sp:Issuer&gt; |</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  &lt;sp:IssuerName&gt;xs:anyURI&lt;/sp:IssuerName&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  ) ?<br>
  &lt;wst:Claims Dialect=&quot;...&quot;&gt; ... &lt;/wst:Claims&gt; ?<br>
  &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp=&quot;...&quot;&gt;<br>
    (<br>
      &lt;sp:RequireDerivedKeys ... /&gt; |<br>
      &lt;sp:RequireImpliedDerivedKeys ... /&gt; |<br>
      &lt;sp:RequireExplicitDerivedKeys ... /&gt; <br>
    ) ?</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>   
&lt;sp:MustNotSendCancel ... /&gt; ?</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>   
&lt;sp:MustNotSendAmend ... /&gt; ?</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>   
&lt;sp:MustNotSendRenew ... /&gt; ?<br>
    ...<br>
  &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt; <br>
  ...<br>
&lt;/sp:SpnegoContextToken&gt;</p>

</div>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined above:</span></p>

<p class=MsoNormal>/sp:SpnegoContextToken</p>

<p class=Definition>This identifies a SpnegoContextToken assertion.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SpnegoContextToken/@sp:IncludeToken</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL attribute identifies the token inclusion
value for this token assertion.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal>/sp:SpnegoContextToken/sp:Issuer</p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element, of type wsa:EndpointReferenceType,
contains a reference to the issuer for the Spnego Context Token.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><a name="_Toc96244148"></a><a name="_Toc95877094"></a><a
name="_Toc95113108"></a><a name="_Toc92518200"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SpnegoContextToken/sp:IssuerName
</span></a></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element, of type xs:anyURI, contains the
logical name of the sp:SpnegoContextToken issuer. </p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SpnegoContextToken/wst:Claims
</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element identifies the REQUIRED claims that a
security token must contain in order to satisfy the token assertion
requirements.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SpnegoContextToken/wsp:Policy</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This REQUIRED element identifies additional requirements
for use of the sp:SpnegoContextToken assertion.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SpnegoContextToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireDerivedKeys</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that sets the
[Derived Keys], [Explicit Derived Keys] and [Implied Derived Keys] properties
for this token to 'true'.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SpnegoContextToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireExplicitDerivedKeys</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that sets the
[Derived Keys] and [Explicit Derived Keys] properties for this token to 'true'
and the [Implied Derived Keys] property for this token to 'false'.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SpnegoContextToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireImpliedDerivedKeys</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that sets the
[Derived Keys] and [Implied Derived Keys] properties for this token to 'true'
and the [Explicit Derived Keys] property for this token to 'false'.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:SpnegoContextToken/wsp:Policy/sp:MustNotSendCancel</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the STS issuing the SP/Nego token does not support SCT/Cancel RST
messages. If this assertion is missing it means that SCT/Cancel RST messages
are supported by the STS.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SpnegoContextToken/wsp:Policy/sp:MustNotSendAmend</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the STS issuing the SP/Nego token does not support SCT/Amend RST messages.
If this assertion is missing it means that SCT/Amend RST messages are supported
by the STS.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SpnegoContextToken/wsp:Policy/sp:MustNotSendRenew</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the STS issuing the SP/Nego token does not support SCT/Renew RST messages.
If this assertion is missing it means that SCT/Renew RST messages are supported
by the STS.</p>

<h3><a name="_Toc325572737"></a><a name="_Toc182291516"></a><a
name="_Toc161826540"></a><a name="_Toc104103949"></a><a
name="_SecurityContextToken_Assertion"></a>5.4.6 SecurityContextToken Assertion</h3>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This element
represents a requirement for a SecurityContextToken token.</span></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:
solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in;
background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;sp:SecurityContextToken
sp:IncludeToken=&quot;<i>xs:anyURI</i>&quot;? xmlns:sp=&quot;...&quot; ... &gt;<br>
( </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    &lt;sp:Issuer&gt;wsa:EndpointReferenceType&lt;/sp:Issuer&gt;
|  </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    &lt;sp:IssuerName&gt;xs:anyURI&lt;/sp:IssuerName&gt;
</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  ) ?
</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  &lt;wst:Claims
Dialect=&quot;...&quot;&gt; ... &lt;/wst:Claims&gt; ?</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'> 
&lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp=&quot;...&quot;&gt;<br>
    (<br>
      &lt;sp:RequireDerivedKeys ... /&gt; |<br>
      &lt;sp:RequireImpliedDerivedKeys ... /&gt; |<br>
      &lt;sp:RequireExplicitDerivedKeys ... /&gt; <br>
    ) ?<br>
    &lt;sp:RequireExternalUriReference ... /&gt; ?<br>
    &lt;sp:SC13SecurityContextToken... /&gt; ?<br>
    ...<br>
  &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt; <br>
  ...<br>
&lt;/sp:SecurityContextToken&gt;</p>

</div>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined above:</span></p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'>SecurityContextToken</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This identifies a SecurityContextToken assertion.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecurityContextToken/@sp:IncludeToken</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL attribute identifies the token inclusion
value for this token assertion.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecurityContextToken/sp:Issuer</span>
</p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element, of type wsa:EndpointReferenceType,
contains reference to the issuer of the sp:SecurityContextToken. </p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecurityContextToken/sp:IssuerName
</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element, of type xs:anyURI, contains the
logical name of the sp:SecurityContextToken issuer. </p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecurityContextToken/wst:Claims
</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element identifies the REQUIRED claims that a
security token must contain in order to satisfy the token assertion
requirements.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecurityContextToken/wsp:Policy</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This REQUIRED element identifies additional requirements
for use of the sp:SecurityContextToken assertion.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecurityContextToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireDerivedKeys</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that sets the
[Derived Keys], [Explicit Derived Keys] and [Implied Derived Keys] properties
for this token to 'true'.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecurityContextToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireExplicitDerivedKeys</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that sets the
[Derived Keys] and [Explicit Derived Keys] properties for this token to 'true'
and the [Implied Derived Keys] property for this token to 'false'.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecurityContextToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireImpliedDerivedKeys</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that sets the
[Derived Keys] and [Implied Derived Keys] properties for this token to 'true'
and the [Explicit Derived Keys] property for this token to 'false'.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecurityContextToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireExternalUriReference</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that an external URI reference is REQUIRED when referencing this token.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecurityContextToken/wsp:Policy/sp:SC13SecurityContextToken</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that a Security Context Token SHOULD be used as defined in [<a
href="#wssecureconversation">WS-SecureConversation</a>].</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Note: This
assertion does not describe how to obtain a Security Context Token but rather
assumes that both parties have the token already or have agreed separately on a
mechanism for obtaining the token. If a definition of the mechanism for
obtaining the Security Context Token is desired in policy, then either the
sp:SecureConversationToken or the sp:IssuedToken assertion SHOULD be used
instead.</span></p>

<h3><a name="_Toc325572738"></a><a name="_Toc182291517"></a><a
name="_Toc161826541"></a><a name="_Toc104103950"></a><a name="_Toc99269251"></a><a
name="_SecureConversationToken_Assertion"></a>5.4.7 SecureConversationToken
Assertion</h3>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This element
represents a requirement for a Security Context Token retrieved from the
indicated issuer address. If the sp:Issuer address is absent, the protocol MUST
be executed at the same address as the service endpoint address.</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Note: </span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This </span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'>assertion describes the token accepted by the target
service.  Because this token is issued by the target service and MAY NOT have a
separate port (with separate policy), this assertion SHOULD contain a bootstrap
policy indicating the security binding and policy that is used when requesting
this token from the target service.  That is, the bootstrap policy is used to
obtain the token and then the current (outer) policy is used when making
requests with the token. This is illustrated in the diagram below.</span></p>

<p class=Figure><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><img width=600
height=158 src="ws-securitypolicy-1.2-errata01-os-complete_files/image002.gif"></span></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:
solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in;
background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;sp:SecureConversationToken
sp:IncludeToken=&quot;<i>xs:anyURI</i>&quot;? xmlns:sp=&quot;...&quot; ... &gt;<br>
  (</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'> 
&lt;sp:Issuer&gt;<i>wsa:EndpointReferenceType</i>&lt;/sp:Issuer&gt; |</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  &lt;sp:IssuerName&gt;xs:anyURI&lt;/sp:IssuerName&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  ) ?<br>
  &lt;wst:Claims Dialect=&quot;...&quot;&gt; ... &lt;/wst:Claims&gt; ?<br>
  &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp=&quot;...&quot;&gt;<br>
    (<br>
      &lt;sp:RequireDerivedKeys ... /&gt; |<br>
      &lt;sp:RequireImpliedDerivedKeys ... /&gt; |<br>
      &lt;sp:RequireExplicitDerivedKeys ... /&gt; <br>
    ) ?<br>
    &lt;sp:RequireExternalUriReference ... /&gt; ?<br>
    &lt;sp:SC13SecurityContextToken ... /&gt; ?</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>   
&lt;sp:MustNotSendCancel ... /&gt; ?</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>   
&lt;sp:MustNotSendAmend ... /&gt; ?</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>   
&lt;sp:MustNotSendRenew ... /&gt; ?<br>
    &lt;sp:BootstrapPolicy ... &gt; <br>
      &lt;wsp:Policy&gt; ... &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
    &lt;/sp:BootstrapPolicy&gt; ?<br>
  &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt; <br>
  ...<br>
&lt;/sp:SecureConversationToken&gt;</p>

</div>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined above:</span></p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'>SecureConversationToken</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This identifies a SecureConversationToken assertion.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecureConversationToken/@sp:IncludeToken</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL attribute identifies the token inclusion
value for this token assertion.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal>/sp:SecureConversationToken/sp:Issuer</p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element, of type wsa:EndpointReferenceType,
contains a reference to the issuer for the Security Context Token.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecureConversationToken/sp:IssuerName
</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element, of type xs:anyURI, contains the
logical name of the sp:SecureConversationToken issuer. </p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SpnegoContextToken/wst:Claims
</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element identifies the REQUIRED claims that a
security token must contain in order to satisfy the token assertion
requirements.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecureConversationToken/wsp:Policy</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This REQUIRED element identifies additional requirements
for use of the sp:SecureConversationToken assertion.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecureConversationToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireDerivedKeys</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that sets the
[Derived Keys], [Explicit Derived Keys] and [Implied Derived Keys] properties
for this token to 'true'.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecureConversationToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireExplicitDerivedKeys</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that sets the
[Derived Keys] and [Explicit Derived Keys] properties for this token to 'true'
and the [Implied Derived Keys] property for this token to 'false'.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecureConversationToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireImpliedDerivedKeys</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that sets the
[Derived Keys] and [Implied Derived Keys] properties for this token to 'true'
and the [Explicit Derived Keys] property for this token to 'false'.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecureConversationToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireExternalUriReference</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that an external URI reference is REQUIRED when referencing this token.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecureConversationToken/wsp:Policy/sp:SC13SecurityContextToken</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that a Security Context Token should be used as obtained using the protocol
defined in [<a href="#wssecureconversation">WS-SecureConversation</a>].</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecureConversationToken/wsp:Policy/sp:MustNotSendCancel</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the STS issuing the secure conversation token does not support SCT/Cancel
RST messages. If this assertion is missing it means that SCT/Cancel RST
messages are supported by the STS.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecureConversationToken/wsp:Policy/sp:MustNotSendAmend</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the STS issuing the secure conversation token does not support SCT/Amend
RST messages. If this assertion is missing it means that SCT/Amend RST messages
are supported by the STS.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecureConversationToken/wsp:Policy/sp:MustNotSendRenew</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the STS issuing the secure conversation token does not support SCT/Renew
RST messages. If this assertion is missing it means that SCT/Renew RST messages
are supported by the STS.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal>/sp:SecureConversationToken/wsp:Policy/sp:BootstrapPolicy</p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that contains
the policy indicating the requirements for obtaining the Security Context
Token.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal>/sp:SecureConversationToken/wsp:Policy/sp:BootstrapPolicy/wsp:Policy</p>

<p class=Definition>This element contains the security binding requirements for
obtaining the Security Context Token. It will typically contain a security
binding assertion (e.g. sp:SymmetricBinding) along with protection assertions
(e.g. sp:SignedParts) describing the parts of the RST/RSTR messages that are to
be protected.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Example</span></b></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:
solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in;
background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;wsp:Policy
xmlns:wsp=&quot;...&quot; xmlns:sp=&quot;...&quot;&gt;<br>
  &lt;sp:SymmetricBinding&gt;<br>
    &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
      &lt;sp:ProtectionToken&gt;<br>
        &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
          &lt;sp:SecureConversationToken&gt;<br>
            &lt;sp:Issuer&gt;<br>
              &lt;wsa:Address&gt;http://example.org/sts&lt;/wsa:Address&gt;<br>
            &lt;/sp:Issuer&gt;<br>
            &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>             
&lt;sp:SC13SecurityContextToken /&gt;<br>
              &lt;sp:BootstrapPolicy&gt;<br>
                &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
                  &lt;sp:AsymmetricBinding&gt;<br>
                    &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
                      &lt;sp:InitiatorToken&gt;<br>
                        ...<br>
                      &lt;/sp:InitiatorToken&gt;<br>
                      &lt;sp:RecipientToken&gt;<br>
                        ...<br>
                      &lt;/sp:RecipientToken&gt;<br>
                    &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
                  &lt;/sp:AsymmetricBinding&gt;<br>
                  &lt;sp:SignedParts&gt;<br>
                  ...<br>
                  &lt;/sp:SignedParts&gt;<br>
                  ...<br>
                &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
              &lt;/sp:BootstrapPolicy&gt;<br>
            &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
          &lt;/sp:SecureConversationToken&gt;<br>
        &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
      &lt;/sp:ProtectionToken&gt;<br>
      ...<br>
    &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
  &lt;/sp:SymmetricBinding&gt;<br>
  &lt;sp:SignedParts&gt;<br>
  ...<br>
  &lt;/sp:SignedParts&gt;<br>
  ...<br>
&lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;</p>

</div>

<h3><a name="_Toc325572739"></a><a name="_Toc182291518"></a><a
name="_Toc161826542"></a><a name="_Toc104103951"></a><a
name="_SamlToken_Assertion"></a>5.4.8 SamlToken Assertion</h3>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This element
represents a requirement for a SAML token.</span></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:
solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in;
background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;sp:SamlToken
sp:IncludeToken=&quot;<i>xs:anyURI</i>&quot;? xmlns:sp=&quot;...&quot; ... &gt;<br>
  ( </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    &lt;sp:Issuer&gt;wsa:EndpointReferenceType&lt;/sp:Issuer&gt;
|  </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    &lt;sp:IssuerName&gt;xs:anyURI&lt;/sp:IssuerName&gt;
</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  ) ?
</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  &lt;wst:Claims
Dialect=&quot;...&quot;&gt; ... &lt;/wst:Claims&gt; ?</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'> 
&lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp=&quot;...&quot;&gt;<br>
    (<br>
      &lt;sp:RequireDerivedKeys ... /&gt; |<br>
      &lt;sp:RequireImpliedDerivedKeys ... /&gt; |<br>
      &lt;sp:RequireExplicitDerivedKeys ... /&gt; <br>
    ) ?<br>
    &lt;sp:RequireKeyIdentifierReference ... /&gt; ?<br>
    (<br>
      &lt;sp:WssSamlV11Token10 ... /&gt; |<br>
      &lt;sp:WssSamlV11Token11 ... /&gt; |<br>
      &lt;sp:WssSamlV20Token11 ... /&gt;<br>
    ) ?</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>   
...<br>
  &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt; <br>
  ...<br>
&lt;/sp:SamlToken&gt;</p>

</div>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following describes
the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined above:</span></p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span

"Arial","sans-serif"'>SamlToken</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This identifies a SamlToken assertion.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SamlToken/@sp:IncludeToken</span></p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SamlToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireDerivedKeys</span></p>
