<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SamlToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireImpliedDerivedKeys</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that sets the
[Derived Keys] and [Implied Derived Keys] properties for this token to 'true'
and the [Explicit Derived Keys] property for this token to 'false'.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SamlToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireKeyIdentifierReference</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that a key identifier reference is REQUIRED when referencing this token.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SamlToken/wsp:Policy/sp:WssSamlV11Token10</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that identifies
that a SAML Version 1.1 token should be used as defined in [<a href="#wsssaml10">WSS:SAMLTokenProfile1.0</a>].</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SamlToken/wsp:Policy/sp:WssSamlV11Token11</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that identifies
that a SAML Version 1.1 token should be used as defined in [<a href="#wsssaml11">WSS:SAMLTokenProfile1.1</a>].</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SamlToken/wsp:Policy/sp:WssSamlV20Token11</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that identifies
that a SAML Version 2.0 token should be used as defined in [<a href="#wsssaml11">WSS:SAMLTokenProfile1.1</a>].</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Note: This
assertion does not describe how to obtain a SAML Token but rather assumes that
both parties have the token already or have agreed separately on a mechanism
for obtaining the token. If a definition of the mechanism for obtaining the
SAML Token is desired in policy, the sp:IssuedToken assertion SHOULD be used
instead. </span></p>

<h3><a name="_Toc325572740"></a><a name="_Toc182291519"></a><a
name="_Toc161826543"></a><a name="_Toc104103952"></a><a
name="_RelToken_Assertion"></a>5.4.9 RelToken Assertion</h3>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This element
represents a requirement for a REL token.</span></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:
solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in;
background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;sp:RelToken
sp:IncludeToken=&quot;<i>xs:anyURI</i>&quot;? xmlns:sp=&quot;...&quot; ... &gt;<br>
  ( </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    &lt;sp:Issuer&gt;wsa:EndpointReferenceType&lt;/sp:Issuer&gt;
|  </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    &lt;sp:IssuerName&gt;xs:anyURI&lt;/sp:IssuerName&gt;
</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  ) ?
</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  &lt;wst:Claims
Dialect=&quot;...&quot;&gt; ... &lt;/wst:Claims&gt; ?</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'> 
&lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp=&quot;...&quot;&gt;<br>
    (<br>
      &lt;sp:RequireDerivedKeys ... /&gt; |<br>
      &lt;sp:RequireImpliedDerivedKeys ... /&gt; |<br>
      &lt;sp:RequireExplicitDerivedKeys ... /&gt; <br>
    ) ?<br>
    &lt;sp:RequireKeyIdentifierReference ... /&gt; ?<br>
    (<br>
      &lt;sp:WssRelV10Token10 ... /&gt; |<br>
      &lt;sp:WssRelV20Token10 ... /&gt; |<br>
      &lt;sp:WssRelV10Token11 ... /&gt; |<br>
      &lt;sp:WssRelV20Token11 ... /&gt;<br>
    ) ?<br>
    ...<br>
  &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt; <br>
  ...<br>
&lt;/sp:RelToken&gt;</p>

</div>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined above:</span></p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'>RelToken</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This identifies a RelToken assertion.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RelToken/@sp:IncludeToken</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL attribute identifies the token inclusion
value for this token assertion.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RelToken/sp:Issuer</span>
</p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element, of type wsa:EndpointReferenceType,
contains reference to the issuer of the sp:RelToken. </p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RelToken/sp:IssuerName
</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element, of type xs:anyURI, contains the
logical name of the sp:RelToken issuer. </p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RelToken/wst:Claims
</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element identifies the REQUIRED claims that a
security token must contain in order to satisfy the token assertion
requirements.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RelToken/wsp:Policy</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This REQUIRED element identifies additional requirements
for use of the sp:RelToken assertion.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RelToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireDerivedKeys</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that sets the [Derived
Keys], [Explicit Derived Keys] and [Implied Derived Keys] property for this
token to 'true'.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RelToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireExplicitDerivedKeys</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that sets the
[Derived Keys] and [Explicit Derived Keys] properties for this token to 'true'
and the [Implied Derived Keys] property for this token to 'false'.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RelToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireImpliedDerivedKeys</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that sets the
[Derived Keys] and [Implied Derived Keys] properties for this token to 'true'
and the [Explicit Derived Keys] property for this token to 'false'.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RelToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireKeyIdentifierReference</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that a key identifier reference is REQUIRED when referencing this token.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RelToken/wsp:Policy/sp:WssRelV10Token10</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that identifies
that a REL Version 1.0 token should be used as defined in [<a href="#wssrel10">WSS:RELTokenProfile1.0</a>].</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RelToken/wsp:Policy/sp:WssRelV20Token10</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that identifies
that a REL Version 2.0 token should be used as defined in [<a href="#wssrel10">WSS:RELTokenProfile1.0</a>].</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RelToken/wsp:Policy/sp:WssRelV10Token11</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that identifies
that a REL Version 1.0 token should be used as defined in [<a href="#wssrel11">WSS:RELTokenProfile1.1</a>].</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RelToken/wsp:Policy/sp:WssRelV20Token11</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that identifies
that a REL Version 2.0 token should be used as defined in [<a href="#wssrel11">WSS:RELTokenProfile1.1</a>].</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Note: This
assertion does not describe how to obtain a REL Token but rather assumes that
both parties have the token already or have agreed separately on a mechanism
for obtaining the token. If a definition of the mechanism for obtaining the REL
Token is desired in policy, the sp:IssuedToken assertion SHOULD be used
instead.</span></p>

<h3><a name="_Toc325572741"></a><a name="_Toc182291520"></a><a
name="_Toc161826544"></a><a name="_Toc104103953"></a><a
name="_HttpsToken_Assertion"></a>5.4.10 HttpsToken Assertion</h3>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This element
represents a requirement for a transport binding to support the use of HTTPS.</span></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:
solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in;
background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;sp:HttpsToken
xmlns:sp=&quot;...&quot; ... &gt;<br>
  ( </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    &lt;sp:Issuer&gt;wsa:EndpointReferenceType&lt;/sp:Issuer&gt;
|  </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    &lt;sp:IssuerName&gt;xs:anyURI&lt;/sp:IssuerName&gt;
</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  ) ?
</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  &lt;wst:Claims
Dialect=&quot;...&quot;&gt; ... &lt;/wst:Claims&gt; ?</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'> 
&lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp=&quot;...&quot;&gt;<br>
    (<br>
      &lt;sp:HttpBasicAuthentication /&gt; |<br>
      &lt;sp:HttpDigestAuthentication /&gt; |<br>
      &lt;sp:RequireClientCertificate /&gt; |<br>
      ...<br>
    )?<br>
    ...<br>
  &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt; <br>
  ...<br>
&lt;/sp:HttpsToken&gt;</p>

</div>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following describes
the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined above:</span></p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'>HttpsToken</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This identifies an Https assertion stating that use of the
HTTPS protocol specification is supported.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><a name="_Toc99269252"></a><a name="_Toc96244149"></a><a
name="_Toc95877095"></a><a name="_Toc95113109"></a><a name="_Toc92518201"><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:HttpsToken/sp:Issuer</span> </a></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element, of type wsa:EndpointReferenceType,
contains reference to the issuer of the sp:HttpsToken. </p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:HttpsToken/sp:IssuerName
</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element, of type xs:anyURI, contains the
logical name of the sp:HttpsToken issuer. </p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:HttpsToken/wst:Claims
</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element identifies the REQUIRED claims that a
security token must contain in order to satisfy the token assertion
requirements.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:HttpsToken/wsp:Policy</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This REQUIRED element identifies additional requirements
for use of the sp:HttpsToken assertion.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:HttpsToken/wsp:Policy/sp:HttpBasicAuthentication</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the client MUST use HTTP Basic Authentication [<a href="#rfc2068">RFC2068</a>]
to authenticate to the service.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:HttpsToken/wsp:Policy/sp:HttpDigestAuthentication</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the client MUST use HTTP Digest Authentication [<a href="#rfc2068">RFC2068</a>]
to authenticate to the service.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:HttpsToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireClientCertificate</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the client MUST provide a certificate when negotiating the HTTPS session.</p>

<h3><a name="_Toc325572742"></a><a name="_Toc182291521"></a><a
name="_Toc161826545">5.4.11 KeyValueToken Assertion</a> </h3>

<p class=Default><span style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
element represents a requirement for a KeyValue token. The next section defines
the KeyValue security token abstraction for purposes of this token assertion. </span></p>

<p class=Default><span style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Default><span style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
document defines requirements for KeyValue token when used in combination with
RSA cryptographic algorithm. Additional cryptographic algorithms can be
introduced in other specifications by introducing new nested assertions besides
<i>sp:RsaKeyValue</i>. </span></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span> </b></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:
solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in;
background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;sp:KeyValueToken
sp:IncludeToken=&quot;xs:anyURI&quot;? xmlns:sp=&quot;...&quot; ... &gt; </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'> 
&lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp=&quot;...&quot;&gt; </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    &lt;sp:RsaKeyValue
... /&gt; ? </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>     
... </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'> 
&lt;/wsp:Policy&gt; </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  ...
</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;/sp:KeyValueToken&gt;
</p>

</div>

<p class=Default><span style='font-size:10.0pt'>The following describes the
attributes listed in the schema outlined above: </span></p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:KeyValueToken
</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This identifies a RsaToken assertion. </p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:KeyValueToken/@sp:IncludeToken
</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL attribute identifies the token inclusion
value for this token assertion. </p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:KeyValueToken/wsp:Policy
</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This REQUIRED element identifies additional requirements
for use of the sp:KeyValueToken assertion. </p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:KeyValueToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RsaKeyValue</span><i>
</i></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the ds:RSAKeyValue element must be present in the KeyValue token. This
indicates that an RSA key pair must be used. </p>

<h4>5.4.11.1 KeyValue Token </h4>

<p class=Default><span style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>XML
Signature specification allows reference an arbitrary key pair by using the
corresponding public key value. This allows using an arbitrary key pair to sign
or encrypt XML elements. The purpose of this section is to define the KeyValue
token abstraction that represents such key pair referencing mechanism. </span></p>

<p class=Default><span style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Default><span style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Although
the <i>ds:KeyValue </i>element as defined in the XML Signature specification is
generic enough to be used with any asymmetric cryptographic algorithm this
document only profiles the usage of <i>ds:KeyValue </i>element in combination
with RSA cryptographic algorithm. </span></p>

<p class=Default><span style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Default><span style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The
RSA key pair is represented by the <i>ds:KeyInfo </i>element containing the <i>ds:KeyValue
</i>element with the RSA public key value in <i>ds:RSAKeyValue </i>as defined
in the XML Signature specification: </span></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:
solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in;
background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;ds:KeyInfo
xmlns=&quot;http://www.w3/org/2000/09/xmldsig#&quot;&gt; </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  &lt;ds:KeyValue&gt;
</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    &lt;ds:RSAKeyValue&gt;
</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>     
&lt;ds:Modulus&gt;<i>ds:CryptoBinary</i>&lt;/ds:Modulus&gt; </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>     
&lt;ds:Exponent&gt;<i>ds:CryptoBinary</i>&lt;/ds:Exponent&gt; </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    &lt;/ds:RSAKeyValue&gt;
</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  &lt;ds:KeyValue&gt;
</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;/ds:KeyInfo&gt;
</p>

</div>

<p class=Default><span style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Default><span style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>When
the KeyValue token is used the corresponding public key value appears directly
in the signature or encrypted data <i>ds:KeyInfo </i>element like in the
following example. There is no KeyValue token manifestation outside the <i>ds:KeyInfo
</i>element. </span></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:
solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in;
background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;Signature
xmlns=&quot;http://www.w3.org/2000/09/xmldsig#&quot;&gt; </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  &lt;SignedInfo&gt;
</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    &lt;CanonicalizationMethod
Algorithm=&quot;http://www.w3.org/2001/10/xml-exc-c14n#&quot; /&gt; </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    &lt;SignatureMethod
Algorithm=&quot;http://www.w3.org/2000/09/xmldsig#rsa-sha1&quot; /&gt; </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    &lt;Reference
URI=&quot;#_1&quot;&gt; </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>     
&lt;Transforms&gt; </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>       
&lt;Transform Algorithm=&quot;http://www.w3.org/2001/10/xml-exc-c14n#&quot;
/&gt; </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>     
&lt;/Transforms&gt; </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>     
&lt;DigestMethod Algorithm=&quot;http://www.w3.org/2000/09/xmldsig#sha1&quot;
/&gt; </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>     
&lt;DigestValue&gt;...&lt;/DigestValue&gt; </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    &lt;/Reference&gt;
</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  &lt;/SignedInfo&gt;
</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  &lt;SignatureValue&gt;...&lt;/SignatureValue&gt;
</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><i> 
&lt;KeyInfo&gt; </i></p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><i>   
&lt;KeyValue&gt; </i></p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><i>     
&lt;RSAKeyValue&gt; </i></p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><i>       
&lt;Modulus&gt;...&lt;/Modulus&gt; </i></p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><i>       
&lt;Exponent&gt;...&lt;/Exponent&gt; </i></p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><i>     
&lt;/RSAKeyValue&gt; </i></p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><i>   
&lt;/KeyValue&gt; </i></p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><i> 
&lt;/KeyInfo&gt; </i></p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;/Signature&gt;
</p>

</div>

<p class=Default><span style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Default><span style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Since
there is no representation of the KeyValue token outside the <i>ds:KeyInfo </i>element
and thus no identifier can be associated with the token, the KeyValue token
cannot be referenced by using <i>wsse:SecurityTokenReference </i>element.
However the <i>ds:KeyInfo </i>element representing the KeyValue token can be
used whenever a security token can be used as illustrated on the following
example: </span></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:
solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in;
background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;t:RequestSecurityToken
xmlns:t=&quot;...&quot;&gt; </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  &lt;t:RequestType&gt;...&lt;/t:RequestType&gt;
</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  ...
</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  &lt;t:UseKey&gt;
</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    <i>&lt;KeyInfo
xmlns=&quot;http://www.w3.org/2000/09/xmldsig#&quot;&gt; </i></p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><i>     
&lt;KeyValue&gt; </i></p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><i>       
&lt;RSAKeyValue&gt; </i></p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><i>         
&lt;Modulus&gt;...&lt;/Modulus&gt; </i></p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><i>         
&lt;Exponent&gt;...&lt;/Exponent&gt; </i></p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><i>       
&lt;/RSAKeyValue&gt; </i></p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><i>     
&lt;/KeyValue&gt; </i></p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><i>   
&lt;/KeyInfo&gt; </i></p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  &lt;/t:UseKey&gt;
</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;/t:RequestSecurityToken&gt;</p>

</div>

<div style='border:none;border-top:solid gray 1.0pt;padding:6.0pt 0in 0in 0in'>

<h1><a name="_Toc99269255"></a><a name="_Toc325572743"></a><a
name="_Toc182291522"></a><a name="_Toc161826546"></a><a name="_Toc104103954">6<span
style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span>Security
Binding Properties</a></h1>

</div>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This section
defines the various properties or conditions of a security binding, their
semantics, values and defaults where appropriate. Properties are used by a
binding in a manner similar to how variables are used in code. Assertions
populate, (or set) the value of the property (or variable). When an assertion
that populates a value of a property appears in a policy, that property is set
to the value indicated by the assertion. The security binding then uses the
value of the property to control its behavior. The properties listed here are
common to the various security bindings described in Section 7. Assertions that
define values for these properties are defined in Section 7. The following
properties are used by the security binding assertions.</span></p>

<h2><a name="_Toc96244152"></a><a name="_Toc95877098"></a><a name="_Toc95113095"></a><a
name="_Toc85362766"></a><a name="_Toc85350212"></a><a name="_Toc325572744"></a><a
name="_Toc182291523"></a><a name="_Toc161826547"></a><a name="_Toc104103955">6.1
[Algorithm Suite] Property</a></h2>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This property
specifies the algorithm suite REQUIRED for performing cryptographic operations
with symmetric or asymmetric key based security tokens. An algorithm suite
specifies actual algorithms and allowed key lengths. A policy alternative will
define what algorithms are used and how they are used. This property defines
the set of available algorithms. The value of this property is typically referenced
by a security binding and is used to specify the algorithms used for all
message level cryptographic operations performed under the security binding.</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Note: In some
cases, this property MAY be referenced under a context other than a security
binding and used to control the algorithms used under that context. For
example, supporting token assertions define such a context. In such contexts,
the specified algorithms still apply to message level cryptographic operations.</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>An algorithm suite
defines values for each of the following operations and properties:</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:Symbol'>·<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Sym Sig]         Symmetric
Key Signature</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:Symbol'>·<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Asym Sig]        Signature
with an asymmetric key</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:Symbol'>·<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Dig]                 Digest</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:Symbol'>·<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Enc]                Encryption</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:Symbol'>·<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Sym KW]         Symmetric
Key Wrap</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:Symbol'>·<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Asym KW]       Asymmetric
Key Wrap</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:Symbol'>·<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Comp Key]      Computed
key</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:Symbol'>·<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Enc KD]           Encryption
key derivation</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:Symbol'>·<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Sig KD]           Signature
key derivation</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:Symbol'>·<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Min SKL]          Minimum
symmetric key length</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:Symbol'>·<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Max SKL]         Maximum
symmetric key length</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:Symbol'>·<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Min AKL]          Minimum
asymmetric key length</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:Symbol'>·<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Max AKL]         Maximum
asymmetric key length</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
table provides abbreviations for the algorithm URI used in the table below:</span></p>

<h2><a name="_Toc96244153"></a><a name="_Toc95877099"></a><a name="_Toc95113096"></a><a
name="_Toc85362767"></a><a name="_Toc85350213"></a><a name="_Toc325572745"></a><a
name="_Toc182291524"></a><a name="_Toc161826548"></a><a name="_Toc104103956">6.2
[Timestamp] Property</a></h2>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
property specifies whether a </span><span class=CodeChar><span
style='font-size:9.0pt'>wsu:Timestamp</span></span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'> element is present in the </span><span style='font-family:
"Courier New"'>wsse:Security</span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>
header. If the value is 'true', the timestamp element MUST be present and MUST
be integrity protected either by transport or message level security. If the
value is 'false', the timestamp element MUST NOT be present. The default value
for this property is 'false'.</span></p>

<h2><a name="_Toc325572746"></a><a name="_Toc182291525"></a><a
name="_Toc161826549"></a><a name="_Toc104103957">6.3 [Protection Order]
Property</a></h2>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This property
indicates the order in which integrity and confidentiality are applied to the
message, in cases where both integrity and confidentiality are REQUIRED:</span></p>

<table class=MsoNormalTable border=1 cellspacing=0 cellpadding=0
 style='border-collapse:collapse;border:none'>
 <tr>
  <td width=253 valign=top style='width:190.0pt;border:solid windowtext 1.0pt;
  padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
  <p class=MsoNormal><a name="_Toc99269262">EncryptBeforeSigning</a></p>
  </td>
  <td width=307 valign=top style='width:3.2in;border:solid windowtext 1.0pt;
  border-left:none;padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
  <p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Signature MUST
  computed over ciphertext. Encryption key and signing key MUST be derived from
  the same source key unless distinct keys are provided, see Section 7.5 on the
  AsymmetricBinding.</span></p>
  </td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <td width=253 valign=top style='width:190.0pt;border:solid windowtext 1.0pt;
  border-top:none;padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
  <p class=MsoNormal>SignBeforeEncrypting</p>
  </td>
  <td width=307 valign=top style='width:3.2in;border-top:none;border-left:none;
  border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:solid windowtext 1.0pt;
  padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
  <p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Signature MUST
  be computed over plaintext. The resulting signature SHOULD be encrypted.
  Supporting signatures MUST be over the plain text signature.</span></p>
  </td>
 </tr>
</table>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The default value
for this property is 'SignBeforeEncrypting'.</span></p>

<h2><a name="_Toc325572747"></a><a name="_Toc182291526"></a><a
name="_Toc161826550"></a><a name="_Toc104103958">6.4 [Signature Protection]
Property</a></h2>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
property specifies whether the signature MUST be encrypted. If the value is
'true', the primary signature MUST be encrypted and any signature confirmation
elements MUST also be encrypted. The primary signature element is NOT REQUIRED
to be encrypted if the value is ‘true’ when there is nothing in the message
that is covered by this signature that is encrypted. If the value is 'false',
the primary signature MUST NOT be encrypted and any signature confirmation
elements MUST NOT be encrypted. The default value for this property is 'false'.</span></p>

<h2><a name="_Toc325572748"></a><a name="_Toc182291527"></a><a
name="_Toc161826551"></a><a name="_Toc104103959">6.5 [Token Protection]
Property</a></h2>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
property specifies whether signatures MUST cover the token used to generate
that signature. If the value is 'true', then each token used to generate a
signature MUST be covered by that signature. If the value is 'false', then the
token MUST NOT be covered by the signature. Note that in cases where derived
keys are used the 'main' token, and NOT the derived key token, is covered by
the signature. It is RECOMMENDED that assertions that define values for this
property apply to [Endpoint Policy Subject]. The default value for this
property is 'false'.</span></p>

<h2><a name="_Toc325572749"></a><a name="_Toc182291528"></a><a
name="_Toc161826552"></a><a name="_Toc104103960">6.6 [Entire Header and Body
Signatures] Property</a></h2>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
property specifies whether signature digests over the SOAP body and SOAP
headers MUST only cover the entire body and entire header elements. If the
value is 'true', then each digest over the SOAP body MUST be over the entire
SOAP body element and not a descendant of that element. In addition each digest
over a SOAP header MUST be over an actual header element and not a descendant
of a header element. This restriction does not specifically apply to the
wsse:Security header. However signature digests over child elements of the
wsse:Security header MUST be over the entire child element and not a descendent
of that element. If the value is 'false', then signature digests MAY be over a
descendant of the SOAP Body or a descendant of a header element. Setting the
value of this property to 'true' mitigates against some possible re-writing
attacks. It is RECOMENDDED that assertions that define values for this property
apply to [Endpoint Policy Subject]. The default value for this property is
'false'.</span></p>

<h2><a name="_Toc325572750"></a><a name="_Toc182291529"></a><a
name="_Toc161826553"></a><a name="_Toc104103961">6.7 [Security Header Layout]
Property</a></h2>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This property
indicates which layout rules to apply when adding items to the security header.
The following table shows which rules are defined by this specification.</span></p>

<table class=MsoNormalTable border=1 cellspacing=0 cellpadding=0
 style='border-collapse:collapse;border:none'>
 <tr>
  <td width=253 valign=top style='width:190.0pt;border:solid windowtext 1.0pt;
  padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
  <p class=MsoNormal>Strict</p>
  </td>
  <td width=307 valign=top style='width:3.2in;border:solid windowtext 1.0pt;
  border-left:none;padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
  <p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Items are added
  to the security header following the numbered layout rules described below
  according to a general principle of 'declare before use'.</span></p>
  </td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <td width=253 valign=top style='width:190.0pt;border:solid windowtext 1.0pt;
  border-top:none;padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
  <p class=MsoNormal>Lax</p>
  </td>
  <td width=307 valign=top style='width:3.2in;border-top:none;border-left:none;
  border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:solid windowtext 1.0pt;
  padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
  <p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Items are added
  to the security header in any order that conforms to WSS: SOAP Message
  Security</span></p>
  </td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <td width=253 valign=top style='width:190.0pt;border:solid windowtext 1.0pt;
  border-top:none;padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
  <p class=MsoNormal>LaxTimestampFirst</p>
  </td>
  <td width=307 valign=top style='width:3.2in;border-top:none;border-left:none;
  border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:solid windowtext 1.0pt;
  padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
  <p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>As Lax except
  that the first item in the security header MUST be a wsu:Timestamp. </span><span
  style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Note that the [Timestamp] property
  MUST also be set to 'true' in this case.</span></p>
  </td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <td width=253 valign=top style='width:190.0pt;border:solid windowtext 1.0pt;
  border-top:none;padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
  <p class=MsoNormal>LaxTimestampLast</p>
  </td>
  <td width=307 valign=top style='width:3.2in;border-top:none;border-left:none;
  border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:solid windowtext 1.0pt;
  padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
  <p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>As Lax except
  that the last item in the security header MUST be a wsu:Timestamp. </span><span
  style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Note that the [Timestamp] property
  MUST also be set to 'true' in this case.</span></p>
  </td>
 </tr>
</table>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<h3><a name="_Toc325572751"></a><a name="_Toc182291530"></a><a
name="_Toc161826554"></a><a name="_Toc104103962">6.7.1 Strict Layout Rules</a>
for WSS 1.0</h3>

<ol style='margin-top:0in' start=1 type=1>
 <li class=MsoNormal>Tokens that are included in the message MUST be declared
     before use. For example:</li>
 <ol style='margin-top:0in' start=1 type=a>
  <li class=MsoNormal>A local signing token MUST occur before the signature
      that uses it.</li>
  <li class=MsoNormal>A local token serving as the source token for a derived
      key token MUST occur before that derived key token.</li>
  <li class=MsoNormal>A local encryption token MUST occur before the reference
      list that points to xenc:EncryptedData elements that use it.</li>
  <li class=MsoNormal>If the same token is used for both signing and
      encryption, then it SHOULD appear before the ds:Signature and
      xenc:ReferenceList elements in the security header that are generated
      using the token.</li>
 </ol>
 <li class=MsoNormal>Signed elements inside the security header MUST occur
     before the signature that signs them.  For example:</li>
 <ol style='margin-top:0in' start=1 type=a>
  <li class=MsoNormal>A timestamp MUST occur before the signature that signs
      it.</li>
  <li class=MsoNormal>A Username token (usually in encrypted form) MUST occur
      before the signature that signs it.</li>
  <li class=MsoNormal>A primary signature MUST occur before the supporting
      token signature that signs the primary signature's signature value
      element.</li>
 </ol>
 <li class=MsoNormal>When an element in a security header is encrypted, the
     resulting xenc:EncryptedData element has the same order requirements as
     the source plain text element, unless requirement 4 indicates otherwise.
     For example, an encrypted primary signature MUST occur before any
     supporting token signature per 2.c above and an encrypted token has the
     same ordering requirements as the unencrypted token.</li>
</ol>

<p class=MsoNormal>If there are any encrypted elements in the message then a
top level xenc:ReferenceList element or a top level xenc:EncryptedKey element
which contains an xenc:ReferenceList element MUST be present in the security
header. The xenc:ReferenceList or xenc:EncryptedKey MUST occur before any
xenc:EncryptedData elements in the security header that are referenced from the
reference list. Strict Layout Rules for WSS 1.1</p>

<ol style='margin-top:0in' start=1 type=1>
 <li class=MsoNormal>Tokens that are included in the message MUST be declared
     before use. For example:</li>
 <ol style='margin-top:0in' start=1 type=a>
  <li class=MsoNormal>A local signing token MUST occur before the signature
      that uses it.</li>
  <li class=MsoNormal>A local token serving as the source token for a derived
      key token MUST occur before that derived key token.</li>
  <li class=MsoNormal>A local encryption token MUST occur before the reference
      list that points to xenc:EncryptedData elements that use it.</li>
  <li class=MsoNormal>If the same token is used for both signing and
      encryption, then it SHOULD appear before the ds:Signature and
      xenc:ReferenceList elements in the security header that are generated
      using the token.</li>
 </ol>
 <li class=MsoNormal>Signed elements inside the security header MUST occur
     before the signature that signs them.  For example:</li>
 <ol style='margin-top:0in' start=1 type=a>
  <li class=MsoNormal>A timestamp MUST occur before the signature that signs
      it.</li>
  <li class=MsoNormal>A Username token (usually in encrypted form) MUST occur
      before the signature that signs it.</li>
  <li class=MsoNormal>A primary signature MUST occur before the supporting
      token signature that signs the primary signature's signature value
      element.</li>
  <li class=MsoNormal>A wsse11:SignatureConfirmation element MUST occur before
      the signature that signs it.</li>
 </ol>
 <li class=MsoNormal>When an element in a security header is encrypted, the
     resulting xenc:EncryptedData element has the same order requirements as
     the source plain text element, unless requirement 4 indicates otherwise.
     For example, an encrypted primary signature MUST occur before any
     supporting token signature per 2.c above and an encrypted token has the
     same ordering requirements as the unencrypted token.</li>
 <li class=MsoNormal>If there are any encrypted elements in the message then a
     top level xenc:ReferenceList element MUST be present in the security
     header. The xenc:ReferenceList MUST occur before any xenc:EncryptedData
     elements in the security header that are referenced from the reference
     list. However, the xenc:ReferenceList is NOT REQUIRED to appear before
     independently encrypted tokens such as the xenc:EncryptedKey token as
     defined in WSS.</li>
 <li class=MsoNormal>An xenc:EncryptedKey element without an internal reference
     list [<a href="#wss11">WSS: SOAP Message Security 1.1</a>] MUST obey rule
     1 above.</li>
</ol>

<div style='border:none;border-top:solid gray 1.0pt;padding:6.0pt 0in 0in 0in'>

<h1><a name="_Toc325572752"></a><a name="_Toc182291531"></a><a
name="_Toc161826555"></a><a name="_Toc104103963"></a><a name="_Toc96244166"></a><a
name="_Toc95877112"></a><a name="_Toc95113117"></a><a name="_Toc85362795"></a><a
name="_Toc85350240">7<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span>Security Binding Assertions</a></h1>

</div>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The appropriate
representation of the different facets of security mechanisms requires
distilling the common primitives (to enable reuse) and then combining the
primitive elements into patterns. The policy scope of assertions defined in
this section is the policy scope of their containing element.</span></p>

<h2><a name="_Toc96244167"></a><a name="_Toc95877113"></a><a name="_Toc95113118"></a><a
name="_Toc85362796"></a><a name="_Toc85350241"></a><a name="_Toc73939793"></a><a
name="_Toc325572753"></a><a name="_Toc182291532"></a><a name="_Toc161826556"></a><a
name="_Toc104103964"></a><a name="_AlgorithmSuite_Assertion"></a>7.1 AlgorithmSuite
Assertion</h2>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This assertion
indicates a requirement for an algorithm suite as defined under the [Algorithm
Suite] property described in Section 6.1. The scope of this assertion is
defined by its containing assertion.</span></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:
solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in;
background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;sp:AlgorithmSuite
xmlns:sp=&quot;...&quot; ... &gt;<br>
  &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp=&quot;...&quot;&gt;<br>
   (&lt;sp:Basic256 ... /&gt; |<br>
    &lt;sp:Basic192 ... /&gt; |<br>
    &lt;sp:Basic128 ... /&gt; |<br>
    &lt;sp:TripleDes ... /&gt; |<br>
    &lt;sp:Basic256Rsa15 ... /&gt; |<br>
    &lt;sp:Basic192Rsa15 ... /&gt; |<br>
    &lt;sp:Basic128Rsa15 ... /&gt; |<br>
    &lt;sp:TripleDesRsa15 ... /&gt; |<br>
    &lt;sp:Basic256Sha256 ... /&gt; |<br>
    &lt;sp:Basic192Sha256 ... /&gt; |<br>
    &lt;sp:Basic128Sha256 ... /&gt; |<br>
    &lt;sp:TripleDesSha256 ... /&gt; |<br>
    &lt;sp:Basic256Sha256Rsa15 ... /&gt; |<br>
    &lt;sp:Basic192Sha256Rsa15 ... /&gt; |<br>
    &lt;sp:Basic128Sha256Rsa15 ... /&gt; |<br>
    &lt;sp:TripleDesSha256Rsa15 ... /&gt; |<br>
    ...)<br>
    &lt;sp:InclusiveC14N ... /&gt; ?</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    &lt;sp:InclusiveC14N11
... /&gt; ?<br>
    &lt;sp:SOAPNormalization10 ... /&gt; ?<br>
    &lt;sp:STRTransform10 ... /&gt; ?<br>
   (&lt;sp:XPath10 ... /&gt; |<br>
    &lt;sp:XPathFilter20 ... /&gt; |<br>
    &lt;sp:AbsXPath ... /&gt; |<br>
    ...)?<br>
    ...<br>
  &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
  ...<br>
&lt;/sp:AlgorithmSuite&gt;</p>

</div>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined above:</span></p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'>AlgorithmSuite</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This identifies an AlgorithmSuite assertion.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy</p>

<p class=Definition>This REQUIRED element contains one or more policy
assertions that indicate the specific algorithm suite to use.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:Basic256</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [Algorithm Suite] property is set to 'Basic256'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:Basic192</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [Algorithm Suite] property is set to 'Basic192'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:Basic128</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [Algorithm Suite] property is set to 'Basic128'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:TripleDes</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [Algorithm Suite] property is set to 'TripleDes'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:Basic256Rsa15</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [Algorithm Suite] property is set to 'Basic256Rsa15'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:Basic192Rsa15</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [Algorithm Suite] property is set to 'Basic192Rsa15'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:Basic128Rsa15</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [Algorithm Suite] property is set to 'Basic128Rsa15'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:TripleDesRsa15</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [Algorithm Suite] property is set to 'TripleDesRsa15'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:Basic256Sha256</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [Algorithm Suite] property is set to 'Basic256Sha256'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:Basic192Sha256</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [Algorithm Suite] property is set to 'Basic192Sha256'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:Basic128Sha256</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [Algorithm Suite] property is set to 'Basic128Sha256'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:TripleDesSha256</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [Algorithm Suite] property is set to 'TripleDesSha256'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:Basic256Sha256Rsa15</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [Algorithm Suite] property is set to 'Basic256Sha256Rsa15'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:Basic192Sha256Rsa15</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [Algorithm Suite] property is set to 'Basic192Sha256Rsa15'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:Basic128Sha256Rsa15</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [Algorithm Suite] property is set to 'Basic128Sha256Rsa15'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:TripleDesSha256Rsa15</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [Algorithm Suite] property is set to 'TripleDesSha256Rsa15'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:InclusiveC14N</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:InclusiveC14N11</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:SoapNormalization10</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [SOAP Norm] property is set to 'SNT'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:STRTransform10</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:XPath10</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [XPath] property is set to 'XPath'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:XPathFilter20</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:AbsXPath</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [XPath] property is set to 'AbsXPath' (see <a
href="http://www.w3.org/TR/1999/REC-xpath-19991116#NT-AbsoluteLocationPath">AbsoluteLocationPath</a>
in [<a href="#xpath">XPATH</a>]).</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<h2><a name="_Toc325572754"></a><a name="_Toc182291533"></a><a
name="_Toc161826557"></a><a name="_Toc104103965"></a><a name="_Layout_Assertion"></a>7.2
Layout Assertion</h2>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This assertion
indicates a requirement for a particular security header layout as defined
under the [Security Header Layout] property described in Section 6.7. The scope
of this assertion is defined by its containing assertion.</span></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:
solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in;
background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;sp:Layout
xmlns:sp=&quot;...&quot; ... &gt;<br>
  &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp=&quot;...&quot;&gt;<br>
    &lt;sp:Strict ... /&gt; |<br>
    &lt;sp:Lax ... /&gt; |<br>
    &lt;sp:LaxTsFirst ... /&gt; |<br>
    &lt;sp:LaxTsLast ... /&gt; |<br>
    ...<br>
  &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
  ...<br>
&lt;/sp:Layout&gt;</p>

</div>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined above:</span></p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'>Layout</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This identifies a Layout assertion.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal>/sp:Layout/wsp:Policy</p>

<p class=MsoNormal style='margin-left:.25in'>This REQUIRED element contains one
or more policy assertions that indicate the specific security header layout to
use.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Layout/wsp:Policy/sp:Strict</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [Security Header Layout] property is set to 'Strict'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Layout/wsp:Policy/sp:Lax</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [Security Header Layout] property is set to 'Lax'. </p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Layout/wsp:Policy/sp:LaxTsFirst</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [Security Header Layout] property is set to 'LaxTimestampFirst'. Note
that the [Timestamp] property MUST also be set to 'true' by the presence of an
sp:IncludeTimestamp assertion.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Layout/wsp:Policy/sp:LaxTsLast</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [Security Header Layout] property is set to 'LaxTimestampLast'. Note
that the [Timestamp] property MUST also be set to 'true' by the presence of an
sp:IncludeTimestamp assertion.</p>

<h2><a name="_Toc325572755"></a><a name="_Toc182291534"></a><a
name="_Toc161826558"></a><a name="_Toc104103966"></a><a
name="_TransportBinding_Assertion"></a>7.3 TransportBinding Assertion</h2>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The
TransportBinding assertion is used in scenarios in which message protection and
security correlation is provided by means other than </span><a
href="#wssecurity"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WSS: SOAP
Message Security</span></a><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>, for
example by a secure transport like HTTPS.  Specifically, this assertion
indicates that the message is protected using the means provided by the
transport. This binding has one binding specific token property; [Transport
Token]. This assertion MUST apply to [Endpoint Policy Subject].</span></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:
solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in;
background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;sp:TransportBinding
xmlns:sp=&quot;...&quot; ... &gt;<br>
  &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp=&quot;...&quot;&gt;<br>
    &lt;sp:TransportToken ... &gt;<br>
      &lt;wsp:Policy&gt; ... &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
      ...<br>
    &lt;/sp:TransportToken&gt;<br>
    &lt;sp:AlgorithmSuite ... &gt; ... &lt;/sp:AlgorithmSuite&gt;<br>
    &lt;sp:Layout ... &gt; ... &lt;/sp:Layout&gt; ?<br>
    &lt;sp:IncludeTimestamp ... /&gt; ?<br>
    ...<br>
  &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
  ...<br>
&lt;/sp:TransportBinding&gt;</p>

</div>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined above:</span></p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'>TransportBinding</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This identifies a TransportBinding assertion.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:TransportBinding/wsp:Policy</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This indicates a nested <span style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsp:Policy</span>
element that defines the behavior of the TransportBinding assertion.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:TransportBinding/wsp:Policy/sp:TransportToken</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This REQUIRED element is a policy assertion that indicates
a requirement for a Transport Token. The specified token populates the
[Transport Token] property and indicates how the transport is secured.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:TransportBinding/wsp:Policy/sp:TransportToken/wsp:Policy</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This indicates a nested policy that identifies the type of
Transport Token to use.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:TransportBinding/wsp:Policy/sp:AlgorithmSuite</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This REQUIRED element is a policy assertion that indicates
a value that populates the [Algorithm Suite] property. See Section 6.1 for more
details.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:TransportBinding/wsp:Policy/sp:Layout</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
a value that populates the [Security Header Layout] property. See Section 6.7
for more details.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:TransportBinding/wsp:Policy/sp:IncludeTimestamp</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [Timestamp] property is set to 'true'.</p>

<h2><a name="_Toc325572756"></a><a name="_Toc182291535"></a><a
name="_Toc161826559"></a><a name="_Toc104103967"></a><a
name="_SymmetricBinding_Assertion"></a>7.4 SymmetricBinding Assertion</h2>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The
SymmetricBinding assertion is used in scenarios in which message protection is
provided by means defined in </span><a href="#wssecurity"><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WSS: SOAP Message Security</span></a><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>. This binding has two binding
specific token properties; [Encryption Token] and [Signature Token]. If the
message pattern requires multiple messages, this binding defines that the
[Encryption Token] used from initiator to recipient is also used from recipient
to initiator. Similarly, the [Signature Token] used from initiator to recipient
is also use from recipient to initiator. If a sp:ProtectionToken assertion is
specified, the specified token populates both token properties and is used as
the basis for both encryption and signature in both directions. This assertion
SHOULD apply to [Endpoint Policy Subject]. This assertion MAY apply to
[Operation Policy Subject].</span></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:
solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in;
background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;sp:SymmetricBinding
xmlns:sp=&quot;...&quot; ... &gt;<br>
  &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp=&quot;...&quot;&gt;<br>
    (<br>
      &lt;sp:EncryptionToken ... &gt;<br>
        &lt;wsp:Policy&gt; ... &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
      &lt;/sp:EncryptionToken&gt;<br>
      &lt;sp:SignatureToken ... &gt;<br>
        &lt;wsp:Policy&gt; ... &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
      &lt;/sp:SignatureToken&gt;<br>
    ) | (<br>
      &lt;sp:ProtectionToken ... &gt;<br>
        &lt;wsp:Policy&gt; ... &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
      &lt;/sp:ProtectionToken&gt;<br>
    )<br>
    &lt;sp:AlgorithmSuite ... &gt; ... &lt;/sp:AlgorithmSuite&gt;<br>
    &lt;sp:Layout ... &gt; ... &lt;/sp:Layout&gt; ?<br>
    &lt;sp:IncludeTimestamp ... /&gt; ?<br>
    &lt;sp:EncryptBeforeSigning ... /&gt; ?<br>
    &lt;sp:EncryptSignature ... /&gt; ?<br>
    &lt;sp:ProtectTokens ... /&gt; ?<br>
    &lt;sp:OnlySignEntireHeadersAndBody ... /&gt; ?<br>
    ...<br>
  &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
  ...<br>
&lt;/sp:SymmetricBinding&gt;</p>

</div>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined above:</span></p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:SymmetricBinding</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This identifies a SymmetricBinding assertion.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:SymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This indicates a nested wsp:Policy element that defines the
behavior of the SymmetricBinding assertion.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>SymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:EncryptionToken</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
a requirement for an Encryption Token. The specified token populates the
[Encryption Token] property and is used for encryption. It is an error for both
an sp:EncryptionToken and an sp:ProtectionToken assertion to be specified.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>SymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:EncryptionToken/wsp:Policy</span></p>

<p class=Definition>The policy contained here MUST identify exactly one token
to use for encryption.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>SymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy/</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:SignatureToken</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
a requirement for a Signature Token. The specified token populates the
[Signature Token] property and is used for the message signature. It is an
error for both an sp:SignatureToken and an sp:ProtectionToken assertion to be
specified.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>SymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SignatureToken/wsp:Policy</span></p>

<p class=Definition>The policy contained here MUST identify exactly one token
to use for signatures.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>SymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:ProtectionToken</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
a requirement for a Protection Token. The specified token populates the
[Encryption Token] and [Signature Token properties] and is used for the message
signature and for encryption. It is an error for both an sp:ProtectionToken
assertion and either an sp:EncryptionToken assertion or an sp:SignatureToken
assertion to be specified.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>SymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:ProtectionToken/wsp:Policy</span></p>

<p class=Definition>The policy contained here MUST identify exactly one token
to use for protection.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>SymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This REQUIRED element is a policy assertion that indicates
a value that populates the [Algorithm Suite] property. See Section 6.1 for more
details.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SymmetricBinding</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/wsp:Policy</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Layout</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
a value that populates the [Security Header Layout] property. See Section 6.7
for more details.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SymmetricBinding</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/wsp:Policy</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:IncludeTimestamp</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [Timestamp] property is set to 'true'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SymmetricBinding</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/wsp:Policy</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:EncryptBeforeSigning</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [Protection Order] property is set to 'EncryptBeforeSigning'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SymmetricBinding</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/wsp:Policy</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:EncryptSignature</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [Signature Protection] property is set to 'true'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SymmetricBinding</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/wsp:Policy</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:ProtectTokens</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [Token Protection] property is set to 'true'.</p>

<p class=Text><a name="_Toc96244172"></a><a name="_Toc95877118"></a><a
name="_Toc95113123"></a><a name="_Toc85362811"></a><a name="_Toc85350256"></a><a
name="_Toc73939808"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SymmetricBinding</span></a><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/wsp:Policy</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:OnlySignEntireHeadersAndBody</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [Entire Header And Body Signatures] property is set to 'true'.</p>

<h2><a name="_Toc325572757"></a><a name="_Toc182291536"></a><a
name="_Toc161826560"></a><a name="_Toc104103968"></a><a
name="_AsymmetricBinding_Assertion"></a>7.5 AsymmetricBinding Assertion</h2>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The
AsymmetricBinding assertion is used in scenarios in which message protection is
provided by means defined in WSS: SOAP Message Security using asymmetric key
(Public Key) technology. Commonly used asymmetric algorithms, such as RSA,
allow the same key pair to be used for both encryption and signature. However
it is also common practice to use distinct keys for encryption and signature,
because of their different lifecycles. </span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This binding
enables either of these practices by means of four binding specific token
properties: [Initiator Signature Token], [Initiator Encryption Token],
[Recipient Signature Token] and [Recipient Encryption Token]. </span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If the same key
pair is used for signature and encryption, then [Initiator Signature Token] and
[Initiator Encryption Token] will both refer to the same token. Likewise
[Recipient Signature Token] and [Recipient Encryption Token] will both refer to
the same token. </span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If distinct key
pairs are used for signature and encryption then [Initiator Signature Token]
and [Initiator Encryption Token] will refer to different tokens. Likewise
[Recipient Signature Token] and [Recipient Encryption Token] will refer to
different tokens. </span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If the message
pattern requires multiple messages, the [Initiator Signature Token] is used for
the message signature from initiator to the recipient. The [Initiator Encryption
Token] is used for the response message encryption from recipient to the
initiator. The [Recipient Signature Token] is used for the response message
signature from recipient to the initiator. The [Recipient Encryption Token] is
used for the message encryption from initiator to the recipient. Note that in
each case, the token is associated with the party (initiator or recipient) who
knows the secret. </span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This assertion
SHOULD apply to [Endpoint Policy Subject]. This assertion MAY apply to
[Operation Policy Subject].</span></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:
solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in;
background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;sp:AsymmetricBinding
xmlns:sp=&quot;...&quot; ... &gt;<br>
  &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp=&quot;...&quot;&gt;<br>
    ( </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    
&lt;sp:InitiatorToken&gt; <br>
      &lt;wsp:Policy&gt; ... &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
     &lt;/sp:InitiatorToken&gt; </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    )
| ( </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    
&lt;sp:InitiatorSignatureToken&gt; </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>      
&lt;wsp:Policy&gt; ... &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    
&lt;/sp:InitiatorSignatureToken&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    
&lt;sp:InitiatorEncryptionToken&gt; </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>      
&lt;wsp:Policy&gt; ... &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    
&lt;/sp:InitiatorEncryptionToken&gt; </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    )</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    (
</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    
&lt;sp:RecipientToken&gt; <br>
       &lt;wsp:Policy&gt; ... &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
     &lt;/sp:RecipientToken&gt; </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    )
| ( </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    
&lt;sp:RecipientSignatureToken&gt; </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>      
&lt;wsp:Policy&gt; ... &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    
&lt;/sp:RecipientSignatureToken&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    
&lt;sp:RecipientEncryptionToken&gt; </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>      
&lt;wsp:Policy&gt; ... &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    
&lt;/sp:RecipientEncryptionToken&gt; </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    )<br>
    &lt;sp:AlgorithmSuite ... &gt; ... &lt;/sp:AlgorithmSuite&gt;<br>
    &lt;sp:Layout ... &gt; ... &lt;/sp:Layout&gt; ?<br>
    &lt;sp:IncludeTimestamp ... /&gt; ?<br>
    &lt;sp:EncryptBeforeSigning ... /&gt; ?<br>
    &lt;sp:EncryptSignature ... /&gt; ?<br>
    &lt;sp:ProtectTokens ... /&gt; ?<br>
    &lt;sp:OnlySignEntireHeadersAndBody ... /&gt; ?<br>
    ...<br>
  &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
  ...<br>
&lt;/sp:AsymmetricBinding&gt;</p>

</div>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined above:</span></p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:AsymmetricBinding</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This identifies a AsymmetricBinding assertion.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:AsymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This indicates a nested wsp:Policy element that defines the
behavior of the AsymmetricBinding assertion.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>AsymmetricBinding</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/wsp:Policy/sp:InitiatorToken</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
a requirement for an Initiator Token. The specified token populates the
[Initiator Signature Token] and [Initiator Encryption Token] properties and is
used for the message signature from initiator to recipient, and encryption from
