recipient to initiator.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>AsymmetricBinding</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/wsp:Policy/sp:InitiatorToken/wsp:Policy</span></p>

<p class=Definition>The policy contained here MUST identify one or more token
assertions.</p>

<p class=Text><span class=TextChar1><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AsymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy/</span></span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:InitiatorToken/wsp:Policy<span
class=TextChar1><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:InitiatorSignatureToken</span></span></span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
a requirement for an Initiator Signature Token. The specified token populates
the [Initiator Signature Token] property and is used for the message signature
from initiator to recipient.</p>

<p class=Text><span class=TextChar1><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif";
letter-spacing:-.6pt'>/sp:AsymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy/</span></span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif";letter-spacing:-.6pt'>sp:InitiatorToken/wsp:Policy<span
class=TextChar1><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:InitiatorSignatureToken/wsp:Policy</span></span></span></p>

<p class=Definition>The policy contained here MUST identify one or more token
assertions.</p>

<p class=Text><span class=TextChar1><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif";
letter-spacing:-.2pt'>/sp:AsymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy/</span></span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif";letter-spacing:-.2pt'>sp:InitiatorToken/wsp:Policy<span
class=TextChar1><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:InitiatorEncryptionToken</span></span></span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
a requirement for an Initiator Encryption Token. The specified token populates
the [Initiator Encryption Token] property and is used for the message
encryption from recipient to initiator.</p>

<p class=Text><span class=TextChar1><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif";
letter-spacing:-.6pt'>/sp:AsymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy/</span></span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif";letter-spacing:-.6pt'>sp:InitiatorToken/wsp:Policy<span
class=TextChar1><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:InitiatorEncryptionToken/wsp:Policy</span></span></span></p>

<p class=Definition>The policy contained here MUST identify one or more token
assertions.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>AsymmetricBinding</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/wsp:Policy/sp:RecipientToken</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
a requirement for a Recipient Token. The specified token populates the
[Recipient Signature Token] and [Recipient Encryption Token] property and is
used for encryption from initiator to recipient, and for the message signature
from recipient to initiator.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>AsymmetricBinding</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/wsp:Policy/sp:RecipientToken/wsp:Policy</span></p>

<p class=Definition>The policy contained here MUST identify one or more token
assertions.</p>

<p class=Text><span class=TextChar1><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif";
letter-spacing:-.3pt'>/sp:AsymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy/</span></span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif";letter-spacing:-.3pt'>sp:RecipientToken/wsp:Policy<span
class=TextChar1><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RecipientSignatureToken</span></span></span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
a requirement for a Recipient Signature Token. The specified token populates
the [Recipient Signature Token] property and is used for the message signature
from recipient to initiator.</p>

<p class=Text><span class=TextChar1><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif";
letter-spacing:-.7pt'>/sp:AsymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy/</span></span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif";letter-spacing:-.7pt'>sp:RecipientToken/wsp:Policy<span
class=TextChar1><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RecipientSignatureToken/wsp:Policy</span></span></span></p>

<p class=Definition>The policy contained here MUST identify one or more token
assertions.</p>

<p class=Text><span class=TextChar1><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif";
letter-spacing:-.3pt'>/sp:AsymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy/</span></span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif";letter-spacing:-.3pt'>sp:RecipientToken/wsp:Policy<span
class=TextChar1><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RecipientEncryptionToken</span></span></span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
a requirement for a Recipient Encryption Token. The specified token populates
the [Recipient Encryption Token] property and is used for the message
encryption from initiator to recipient.</p>

<p class=Text><span class=TextChar1><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif";
letter-spacing:-.7pt'>/sp:AsymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy/</span></span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif";letter-spacing:-.7pt'>sp:RecipientToken/wsp:Policy<span
class=TextChar1><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RecipientEncryptionToken/wsp:Policy</span></span></span></p>

<p class=Definition>The policy contained here MUST identify one or more token
assertions.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>AsymmetricBinding</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/wsp:Policy/sp:AlgorithmSuite</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This REQUIRED element is a policy assertion that indicates
a value that populates the [Algorithm Suite] property. See Section 6.1 for more
details.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AsymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy/sp:Layout</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
a value that populates the [Security Header Layout] property. See Section 6.7
for more details.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AsymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy/sp:IncludeTimestamp</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [Timestamp] property is set to 'true'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AsymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy/sp:EncryptBeforeSigning</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [Protection Order] property is set to 'EncryptBeforeSigning'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AsymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy/sp:EncryptSignature</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [Signature Protection] property is set to 'true'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AsymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy/sp:ProtectTokens</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [Token Protection] property is set to 'true'.</p>

<p class=Text><a name="_Toc96244187"></a><a name="_Toc95877133"></a><a
name="_Toc95113138"></a><a name="_Toc85362846"></a><a name="_Toc85350291"><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AsymmetricBinding</span></a><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/wsp:Policy</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:OnlySignEntireHeadersAndBody</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [Entire Header And Body Signatures] property is set to 'true'.</p>

<div style='border:none;border-top:solid gray 1.0pt;padding:6.0pt 0in 0in 0in'>

<h1><a name="_Toc325572758"></a><a name="_Toc182291537"></a><a
name="_Toc161826561"></a><a name="_Toc104103969">8<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span>Supporting Tokens</a></h1>

</div>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Security Bindings
use tokens to secure the message exchange. The Security Binding will require
one to create a signature using the token identified in the Security Binding
policy. This signature will here-to-fore be referred to as the “message
signature”. In case of Transport Binding the message is signed outside of the
message XML by the underlying transport protocol and the signature itself is
not part of the message. Additional tokens MAY be specified to augment the
claims provided by the token associated with the “message signature” provided
by the Security Binding. This section defines seven properties related to
supporting token requirements which MAY be referenced by a Security Binding: [Supporting
Tokens], [Signed Supporting Tokens], [Endorsing Supporting Tokens], [Signed
Endorsing Supporting Tokens], [Signed Encrypted Supporting Tokens], [Endorsing
Encrypted Supporting Tokens] and [Signed Endorsing Encrypted Supporting
Tokens]. Seven assertions are defined to populate those properties:
SupportingTokens, SignedSupportingTokens, EndorsingSupportingTokens,
SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens, SignedEncryptedSupportingTokens,
EndorsingEncryptedSupportingTokens and SignedEndorsingEncryptedSupportingTokens.</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> These assertions SHOULD apply to
[Endpoint Policy Subject]. These assertions MAY apply to [Message Policy
Subject] or [Operation Policy Subject].</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Supporting tokens MAY
be specified at a different scope than the binding assertion which provides support
for securing the exchange. For instance, a binding is specified at the scope of
an endpoint, while the supporting tokens might be defined at the scope of a
message. When assertions that populate this property are defined in overlapping
scopes, the sender SHOULD merge the requirements by including all tokens from
the outer scope and any additional tokens for a specific message from the inner
scope. </span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>In cases where
multiple tokens are specified that sign and/or encrypt overlapping message
parts, all the tokens SHOULD sign and encrypt the various message parts. In
such cases ordering of elements (tokens, signatures, reference lists etc.) in
the security header would be used to determine which order signature and
encryptions occurred in.</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Policy authors
need to ensure that the tokens they specify as supporting tokens can satisfy
any additional constraints defined by the supporting token assertion. For
example, if the supporting token assertion specifies message parts that need to
be encrypted, the specified tokens need to be capable of encryption.</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>To illustrate the
different ways that supporting tokens MAY be bound to the message, let’s
consider a message with three components: Header1, Header2, and Body.</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Figure><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><img border=0
width=197 height=125
src="ws-securitypolicy-1.2-errata01-os-complete_files/image003.gif"></span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Even before any supporting
tokens are added, each binding requires that the message is signed using a
token satisfying the REQUIRED usage for that binding, and that the signature
(Sig1) covers important parts of the message including the message timestamp
(TS) facilitate replay detection. The signature is then included as part of the
Security header as illustrated below:</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Figure><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><img border=0
width=197 height=209
src="ws-securitypolicy-1.2-errata01-os-complete_files/image004.gif"></span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Note: if REQUIRED,
the initiator may also include in the Security header the token used as the
basis for the message signature (Sig1), not shown in the diagram.</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If transport
security is used, only the message timestamp (TS) is included in the Security
header as illustrated below. The “message signature” is provided by the
underlying transport protocol and is not part of the message XML.</span></p>

<p class=Figure><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><img border=0
width=197 height=185
src="ws-securitypolicy-1.2-errata01-os-complete_files/image005.gif"></span></p>

<h2><a name="_Toc325572759"></a><a name="_Toc182291538"></a><a
name="_Toc161826562"></a><a name="_Toc104103970"></a><a
name="_SupportingTokens_Assertion"></a>8.1 SupportingTokens Assertion</h2>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Supporting tokens
are included in the security header and MAY OPTIONALLY include additional
message parts to sign and/or encrypt. The supporting tokens can be added to any
SOAP message and do not require any protection (signature or encryption) to be
applied to the message before they are added. More specifically there is no
requirement on “message signature” being present before the supporting tokens
are added. However it is RECOMMENDED to employ underlying protection mechanism
to ensure that the supporting tokens are cryptographically bound to the message
during the transmission.</span></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:
solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in;
background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;sp:SupportingTokens
xmlns:sp=&quot;...&quot; ... &gt;<br>
  &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp=&quot;...&quot;&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>   
[Token Assertion]+</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>   
&lt;sp:AlgorithmSuite ... &gt; ... &lt;/sp:AlgorithmSuite&gt; ?</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    (</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>     
&lt;sp:SignedParts ... <span lang=DA>&gt; ... &lt;/sp:SignedParts&gt; |</span></p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><span
lang=DA>      &lt;sp:SignedElements ... &gt; ... &lt;/sp:SignedElements&gt; |</span></p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><span
lang=DA>      </span>&lt;sp:EncryptedParts ... &gt; ...
&lt;/sp:EncryptedParts&gt; |</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>     
&lt;sp:EncryptedElements ... &gt; ... &lt;/sp:EncryptedElements&gt; |</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>     
&lt;sp:ContentEncryptedElements ... &gt; ...
&lt;/sp:ContentEncryptedElements&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    )
*</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>   
...</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'> 
&lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  ...<br>
&lt;/sp:SupportingTokens&gt;</p>

</div>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined above:</span></p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'>SupportingTokens</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This identifies a SupportingTokens assertion. The specified
tokens populate the [Supporting Tokens] property.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SupportingTokens/wsp:Policy</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This describes additional requirements for satisfying the
SupportingTokens assertion.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/[Token
Assertion]</span></p>

<p class=Definition>The policy MUST identify one or more token assertions.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:AlgorithmSuite</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that follows
the schema outlined in Section 7.1 and describes the algorithms to use for
cryptographic operations performed with the tokens identified by this policy
assertion.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:SignedParts</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that follows
the schema outlined in Section 4.1.1 and describes additional message parts
that MUST be included in the signature generated with the token identified by
this policy assertion.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:SignedElements</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that follows
the schema outlined in Section 4.1.2 and describes additional message elements
that MUST be included in the signature generated with the token identified by
this policy assertion.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:EncryptedParts</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that follows
the schema outlined in Section 4.2.1 and describes additional message parts
that MUST be encrypted using the token identified by this policy assertion.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:EncryptedElements</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that follows
the schema outlined in Section 4.2.2 and describes additional message elements
that MUST be encrypted using the token identified by this policy assertion.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:ContentEncryptedElements
</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that follows
the schema outlined in Section 4.2.3 and describes additional message elements
whose content MUST be encrypted using the token identified by this policy
assertion.</p>

<p class=Definitionterm>&nbsp;</p>

<h2><a name="_Toc325572760"></a><a name="_Toc182291539"></a><a
name="_Toc161826563"></a><a name="_Toc104103971"></a><a
name="_SignedSupportingTokens_Assertion"></a>8.2 SignedSupportingTokens
Assertion</h2>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Signed tokens are
included in the “message signature” as defined above</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> and MAY OPTIONALLY include additional
message parts to sign and/or encrypt</span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>.
The diagram below illustrates how the attached token (Tok2) is signed by the
message signature (Sig1):</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Figure><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><img border=0
width=197 height=233
src="ws-securitypolicy-1.2-errata01-os-complete_files/image006.gif"> </span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If transport
security is used, the token (Tok2) is included in the Security header as
illustrated below:</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Figure><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><img border=0
width=197 height=209
src="ws-securitypolicy-1.2-errata01-os-complete_files/image007.gif"></span></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:
solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in;
background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;sp:SignedSupportingTokens
xmlns:sp=&quot;...&quot; ... &gt;<br>
  &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp=&quot;...&quot;&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>   
[Token Assertion]+</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    &lt;sp:AlgorithmSuite
... &gt; ... &lt;/sp:AlgorithmSuite&gt; ?</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    (</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>     
&lt;sp:SignedParts ... <span lang=DA>&gt; ... &lt;/sp:SignedParts&gt; |</span></p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><span
lang=DA>      &lt;sp:SignedElements ... &gt; ... &lt;/sp:SignedElements&gt; |</span></p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><span
lang=DA>      </span>&lt;sp:EncryptedParts ... &gt; ...
&lt;/sp:EncryptedParts&gt; |</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>     
&lt;sp:EncryptedElements ... &gt; ... &lt;/sp:EncryptedElements&gt; |</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>     
&lt;sp:ContentEncryptedElements ... &gt; ...
&lt;/sp:ContentEncryptedElements&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    )
*</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>   
...</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'> 
&lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  ...<br>
&lt;/sp:SignedSupportingTokens&gt;</p>

</div>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined above:</span></p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'>SignedSupportingTokens</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This identifies a SignedSupportingTokens assertion. The
specified tokens populate the [Signed Supporting Tokens] property.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SignedSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This describes additional requirements for satisfying the
SignedSupportingTokens assertion.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SignedSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/[Token
Assertion]</span></p>

<p class=Definition>The policy MUST identify one or more token assertions.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SignedSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:AlgorithmSuite</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that follows
the schema outlined in Section 7.1 and describes the algorithms to use for
cryptographic operations performed with the tokens identified by this policy
assertion.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SignedSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:SignedParts</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that follows
the schema outlined in Section 4.1.1 and describes additional message parts
that MUST be included in the signature generated with the token identified by
this policy assertion.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SignedSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:SignedElements</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that follows
the schema outlined in Section 4.1.2 and describes additional message elements
that MUST be included in the signature generated with the token identified by
this policy assertion.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SignedSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:EncryptedParts</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that follows
the schema outlined in Section 4.2.1 and describes additional message parts
that MUST be encrypted using the token identified by this policy assertion.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SignedSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:EncryptedElements</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that follows
the schema outlined in Section 4.2.2 and describes additional message elements
that MUST be encrypted using the token identified by this policy assertion.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SignedSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:ContentEncryptedElements
</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that follows
the schema outlined in Section 4.2.3 and describes additional message elements
whose content MUST be encrypted using the token identified by this policy
assertion.</p>

<h2><a name="_Toc325572761"></a><a name="_Toc182291540"></a><a
name="_Toc161826564"></a><a name="_Toc104103972"></a><a
name="_EndorsingSupportingTokens_Assertion"></a>8.3 EndorsingSupportingTokens
Assertion</h2>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Endorsing tokens
sign the message signature, that is they sign the entire </span><span
style='font-family:"Courier New"'>ds:Signature</span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'> element produced from the message signature</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> and MAY OPTIONALLY include additional
message parts to sign and/or encrypt</span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>.
The diagram below illustrates how the endorsing signature (Sig2) signs the
message signature (Sig1):</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Figure><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><img border=0
width=197 height=233
src="ws-securitypolicy-1.2-errata01-os-complete_files/image008.gif"></span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If transport
security is used, the signature (Sig2) MUST cover the message timestamp as
illustrated below:</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Figure><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><img border=0
width=197 height=209
src="ws-securitypolicy-1.2-errata01-os-complete_files/image009.gif"></span></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:
solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in;
background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;sp:EndorsingSupportingTokens
xmlns:sp=&quot;...&quot; ... &gt;<br>
  &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp=&quot;...&quot;&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>   
[Token Assertion]+</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>   
&lt;sp:AlgorithmSuite ... &gt; ... &lt;/sp:AlgorithmSuite&gt; ?</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    (</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>     
&lt;sp:SignedParts ... <span lang=DA>&gt; ... &lt;/sp:SignedParts&gt; |</span></p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><span
lang=DA>      &lt;sp:SignedElements ... &gt; ... &lt;/sp:SignedElements&gt; |</span></p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><span
lang=DA>      </span>&lt;sp:EncryptedParts ... &gt; ...
&lt;/sp:EncryptedParts&gt; |</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>     
&lt;sp:EncryptedElements ... &gt; ... &lt;/sp:EncryptedElements&gt; |</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>     
&lt;sp:ContentEncryptedElements ... &gt; ... &lt;/sp:ContentEncryptedElements&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    )
*</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>   
...</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'> 
&lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  ...<br>
&lt;/sp:EndorsingSupportingTokens&gt;</p>

</div>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined above:</span></p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'>EndorsingSupportingTokens</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This identifies an EndorsingSupportingTokens assertion. The
specified tokens populate the [Endorsing Supporting Tokens] property.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:EndorsingSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This describes additional requirements for satisfying the
EndorsingSupportingTokens assertion.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:EndorsingSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/[Token
Assertion]</span></p>

<p class=Definition>The policy MUST identify one or more token assertions.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:EndorsingSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:AlgorithmSuite</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that follows
the schema outlined in Section 7.1 and describes the algorithms to use for cryptographic
operations performed with the tokens identified by this policy assertion.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:EndorsingSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:SignedParts</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that follows
the schema outlined in Section 4.1.1 and describes additional message parts
that MUST be included in the signature generated with the token identified by
this policy assertion.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:EndorsingSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:SignedElements</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that follows
the schema outlined in Section 4.1.2 and describes additional message elements
that MUST be included in the signature generated with the token identified by
this policy assertion.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:EndorsingSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:EncryptedParts</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that follows
the schema outlined in Section 4.2.1 and describes additional message parts
that MUST be encrypted using the token identified by this policy assertion.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:EndorsingSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:EncryptedElements</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that follows
the schema outlined in Section 4.2.2 and describes additional message elements
that MUST be encrypted using the token identified by this policy assertion.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:EndorsingSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:ContentEncryptedElements
</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that follows
the schema outlined in Section 4.2.3 and describes additional message elements
whose content MUST be encrypted using the token identified by this policy
assertion.</p>

<h2><a name="_Toc104103973"></a><a name="_SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens_Ass"></a>8.4
 <a name="_Toc325572762"></a><a name="_Toc182291541"></a><a name="_Toc161826565">SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens
Assertion</a></h2>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Signed endorsing
tokens sign the entire </span><span style='font-family:"Courier New"'>ds:Signature</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> element produced from the message
signature and are themselves signed by that message signature, that is both
tokens (the token used for the message signature and the signed endorsing token)
sign each other. This assertion </span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>MAY
OPTIONALLY include additional message parts to sign and/or encrypt. </span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The diagram below illustrates how the
signed token (Tok2) is signed by the message signature (Sig1) and the endorsing
signature (Sig2) signs the message signature (Sig1):</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Figure><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><img border=0
width=197 height=257
src="ws-securitypolicy-1.2-errata01-os-complete_files/image010.gif"></span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If transport
security is used, the token (Tok2) is included in the Security header and the
signature (Sig2) SHOULD cover the message timestamp as illustrated below:</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Figure><i><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><img
border=0 width=197 height=233
src="ws-securitypolicy-1.2-errata01-os-complete_files/image011.gif"></span></i></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:
solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in;
background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens
xmlns:sp=&quot;...&quot; ... &gt;<br>
  &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp=&quot;...&quot;&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>   
[Token Assertion]+</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>   
&lt;sp:AlgorithmSuite ... &gt; ... &lt;/sp:AlgorithmSuite&gt; ?</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    (</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>     
&lt;sp:SignedParts ... <span lang=DA>&gt; ... &lt;/sp:SignedParts&gt; |</span></p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><span
lang=DA>      &lt;sp:SignedElements ... &gt; ... &lt;/sp:SignedElements&gt; |</span></p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><span
lang=DA>      </span>&lt;sp:EncryptedParts ... &gt; ...
&lt;/sp:EncryptedParts&gt; |</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>     
&lt;sp:EncryptedElements ... &gt; ... &lt;/sp:EncryptedElements&gt; |</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>     
&lt;sp:ContentEncryptedElements ... &gt; ...
&lt;/sp:ContentEncryptedElements&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>    )
*</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>   
...</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'> 
&lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  ...<br>
&lt;/sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens&gt;</p>

</div>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined above:</span></p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'>SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This identifies a SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens assertion.
The specified tokens populate the [Signed Endorsing Supporting Tokens]
property.</p>

<p class=Text><a name="_Toc99269276"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy</span></a></p>

<p class=Definition>This describes additional requirements for satisfying the
EndorsingSupportingTokens assertion.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/[Token
Assertion]</span></p>

<p class=Definition>The policy MUST identify one or more token assertions.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:AlgorithmSuite</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that follows
the schema outlined in Section 7.1 and describes the algorithms to use for
cryptographic operations performed with the tokens identified by this policy
assertion.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:SignedParts</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that follows
the schema outlined in Section 4.1.1 and describes additional message parts
that MUST be included in the signature generated with the token identified by
this policy assertion.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:SignedElements</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element follows the schema outlined in
Section 4.1.2 and describes additional message elements that MUST be included
in the signature generated with the token identified by this policy assertion.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:EncryptedParts</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that follows
the schema outlined in Section 4.2.1 and describes additional message parts
that MUST be encrypted using the token identified by this policy assertion.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:EncryptedElements</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that follows
the schema outlined in Section 4.2.2 and describes additional message elements
that MUST be encrypted using the token identified by this policy assertion.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:ContentEncryptedElements
</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that follows
the schema outlined in Section 4.2.3 and describes additional message elements
whose content MUST be encrypted using the token identified by this policy
assertion.</p>

<h2><a name="_Toc325572763"></a><a name="_Toc182291542"></a><a
name="_Toc161826566"></a><a name="_SignedEncryptedSupportingTokens_Ass"></a>8.5
SignedEncryptedSupportingTokens Assertion</h2>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Signed, encrypted
supporting tokens are Signed supporting tokens (See</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> </span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>section
8.2) that are also encrypted when they appear in the</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> </span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>wsse:SecurityHeader.
Element Encryption SHOULD be used for encrypting the supporting tokens.</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The syntax for the
sp:SignedEncryptedSupportingTokens differs from the</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> </span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>syntax
of sp:SignedSupportingTokens only in the name of the assertion</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> </span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>itself.
All nested policy is as per the sp:SignedSupportingTokens</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> </span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>assertion.</span></p>

<h2><a name="_Toc325572764"></a><a name="_Toc182291543"></a><a
name="_Toc161826567"></a><a name="_EndorsingEncryptedSupportingTokens_"></a>8.6
EncryptedSupportingTokens Assertion</h2>

<p class=MsoNormal>Encrypted supporting tokens are supporting tokens (See
section 8.1) that are included in the security header and MUST be encrypted
when they appear in the security header. Element encryption SHOULD be used for
encrypting these tokens. The encrypted supporting tokens can be added to any
SOAP message and do not require the “message signature” being present before
the encrypted supporting tokens are added. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal>The syntax for the sp:EncryptedSupportingTokens differs from
the syntax of sp:SupportingTokens only in the name of the assertion itself. All
nested policy is as per the sp:SupportingTokens assertion.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal>The encrypted supporting tokens SHOULD be used only when the
sender cannot provide the “message signature” and it is RECOMMENDED that the
receiver employs some security mechanisms external to the message to prevent
the spoofing attacks. In all other cases it is RECOMMENDED to use signed
encrypted supporting tokens instead to ensure that the encrypted tokens are
cryptographically bound to the message (See section 8.5).</p>

<h2><a name="_Toc325572765"></a><a name="_Toc182291544"></a><a
name="_Toc161826568">8.7 EndorsingEncryptedSupportingTokens Assertion</a></h2>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Endorsing,
encrypted supporting tokens are Endorsing supporting tokens</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> </span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>(See
section 8.3) that are also encrypted when they appear in the</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> </span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>wsse:SecurityHeader.
Element Encryption SHOULD be used for encrypting the supporting tokens.</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The syntax for the
sp:EndorsingEncryptedSupportingTokens differs from</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> </span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>the
syntax of sp:EndorsingSupportingTokens only in the </span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>n</span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>ame
of the</span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> </span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>assertion itself. All nested policy is
as per the</span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> </span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:EndorsingSupportingTokens
assertion.</span></p>

<h2><a name="_Toc325572766"></a><a name="_Toc182291545"></a><a
name="_Toc161826569"></a><a name="_SignedEndorsingEncryptedSupportingT"></a>8.8
SignedEndorsingEncryptedSupportingTokens Assertion</h2>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Signed, endorsing,
encrypted supporting tokens are signed, endorsing</span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'> </span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>supporting
tokens (See section 8.4) that are also encrypted when they</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> </span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>appear
in the wsse:SecurityHeader. Element Encryption SHOULD be used for encrypting the
supporting tokens.</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The syntax for the
sp:SignedEndorsingEncryptedSupportingTokens differs</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> </span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>from
the syntax of sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens only in the name</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> </span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>of
the assertion itself. All nested policy is as per the</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> </span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens
assertion.</span></p>

<h2><a name="_Toc104103974"></a><a name="_Toc325572767"></a><a
name="_Toc182291546"></a><a name="_Toc161826570">8.9 Interaction between [Token
Protection] property and supporting token assertions</a></h2>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If [Token
Protection] (see Section 6.5) is true, then each signature covers the token
that generated that signature and the following statements hold with respect to
the various tokens that sign or are signed;</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:Symbol'>·<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The message
signature, generated from the [Initiator Token] in the Asymmetric Binding case
or the [Signature Token] in the Symmetric binding case, covers that token.</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:Symbol'>·<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Endorsing
signatures cover the main signature and the endorsing token.</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:Symbol'>·<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>For signed,
endorsing supporting tokens, the supporting token is signed twice, once by the
message signature and once by the endorsing signature.</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>In addition,
signed supporting tokens are covered by the message signature, although this is
independent of [Token Protection].</span></p>

<h2><a name="_Toc325572768"></a><a name="_Toc182291547"></a><a
name="_Toc161826571">8.10 Example</a></h2>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Example policy
containing supporting token assertions:</span></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:
solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in;
background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;!--
Example Endpoint Policy --&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;wsp:Policy
xmlns:wsp=&quot;...&quot;&gt;<br>
  &lt;sp:SymmetricBinding xmlns:sp=&quot;...&quot;&gt;<br>
    &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
      &lt;sp:ProtectionToken&gt;<br>
        &lt;sp:IssuedToken sp:IncludeToken=&quot;.../IncludeToken/Once&quot;
&gt;<br>
          &lt;sp:Issuer&gt;...&lt;/sp:Issuer&gt;<br>
          &lt;sp:RequestSecurityTokenTemplate&gt;<br>
          ...<br>
          &lt;/sp:RequestSecurityTokenTemplate&gt;<br>
        &lt;/sp:IssuedToken&gt;<br>
      &lt;/sp:ProtectionToken&gt;<br>
      &lt;sp:AlgorithmSuite&gt;<br>
        &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
          &lt;sp:Basic256 /&gt;<br>
        &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
      &lt;/sp:AlgorithmSuite&gt;<br>
      ...<br>
    &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
  &lt;/sp:SymmetricBinding&gt;<br>
  ...<br>
  &lt;sp:SignedSupportingTokens&gt;<br>
    &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
      &lt;sp:UsernameToken sp:IncludeToken=&quot;.../IncludeToken/Once&quot;
/&gt;<br>
    &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
  &lt;/sp:SignedSupportingTokens&gt;<br>
  &lt;sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens&gt;<br>
    &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
      &lt;sp:X509Token sp:IncludeToken=&quot;.../IncludeToken/Once&quot; &gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>       
&lt;wsp:Policy&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>         
&lt;sp:WssX509v3Token10 /&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>       
&lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>     
&lt;/sp:X509Token&gt;<br>
    &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
  &lt;/sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens&gt;<br>
  ...<br>
&lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;</p>

</div>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The
sp:SignedSupportingTokens assertion in the above policy indicates that a
Username Token must be included in the security header and covered by the
message signature. The sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens assertion indicates
that an X509 certificate must be included in the security header and covered by
the message signature. In addition, a signature over the message signature based
on the key material associated with the X509 certificate must be included in
the security header.</span></p>

<div style='border:none;border-top:solid gray 1.0pt;padding:6.0pt 0in 0in 0in'>

<h1><a name="_Toc73939889"></a><a name="_Toc325572769"></a><a
name="_Toc182291548"></a><a name="_Toc161826572"></a><a name="_Toc104103975"></a><a
name="_Toc96244191"></a><a name="_Toc95877137"></a><a name="_Toc95113142"></a><a
name="_Toc85362849"></a><a name="_Toc85350294"></a><a
name="_12.4._Confidentiality_Requirements"></a><a
name="_12.6._License_Exchange/Issuance_Req"></a>9<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span>WSS: SOAP Message Security Options</h1>

</div>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>There are several OPTIONAL
aspects to the WSS: SOAP Message Security specification that are independent of
the trust and token taxonomies. This section describes another class of
properties and associated assertions that indicate the supported aspects of
WSS: SOAP Message Security.</span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>
The assertions defined here MUST apply to [Endpoint Policy Subject].</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The properties and
assertions dealing with token references defined in this section indicate
whether the initiator and recipient MUST be able to process a given reference
mechanism, or whether the initiator and recipient MAY send a fault if such
references are encountered.</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Note: This
approach is chosen because:</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A)<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[WSS: SOAP Message
Security] allows for multiple equivalent reference mechanisms to be used in a
single reference.</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>B)<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>In a multi-message
exchange, a token MAY be referenced using different mechanisms depending on
which of a series of messages is being secured.</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If a message sent
to a recipient does not adhere to the recipient’s policy the recipient MAY raise
a </span><span style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsse:InvalidSecurity</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> fault.</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><b><u><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WSS: SOAP
Message Security 1.</span></u></b><b><u><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>0
Properties</span></u></b></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Direct
References]</span></b></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This property
indicates whether the initiator and recipient MUST be able to process direct
token references (by ID or URI reference). This property always has a value of
'true'. i.e. All implementations MUST be able to process such references.</span></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></b></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Key Identifier
References]</span></b></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
property indicates whether the initiator and recipient MUST be able to process
key-specific identifier token references. A value of 'true' indicates that the
initiator and recipient MUST be able to generate and process such references. A
value of 'false' indicates that the initiator and recipient MUST NOT generate
such references and that the initiator and recipient MAY send a fault if such
references are encountered.</span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>
This property has a default value of 'false'.</span></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></b></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Issuer Serial
References]</span></b></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
property indicates whether the initiator and recipient MUST be able to process
references using the issuer and token serial number. A value of 'true'
indicates that the initiator and recipient MUST be able to process such
references. A value of 'false' indicates that the initiator and recipient MUST
NOT generate such references and that the initiator and recipient MAY send a
fault if such references are encountered.</span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>
This property has a default value of 'false'.</span></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></b></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[External URI
References]</span></b></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean property
indicates whether the initiator and recipient MUST be able to process
references to tokens outside the message using URIs. A value of 'true'
indicates that the initiator and recipient MUST be able to process such
references. A value of 'false' indicates that the initiator and recipient MUST
NOT generate such references and that the initiator and recipient MAY send a
fault if such references are encountered. </span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
property has a default value of 'false'.</span></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Embedded Token
References]</span></b></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
property indicates whether the initiator and recipient MUST be able to process
references that contain embedded tokens. A value of 'true' indicates that the
initiator and recipient MUST be able to process such references. A value of
'false' indicates that the initiator and recipient MUST NOT generate such
references and that the initiator and recipient MAY send a fault if such
references are encountered.</span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>
This property has a default value of 'false'.</span></p>

<p class=Text><b><u><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><span
 style='text-decoration:none'>&nbsp;</span></span></u></b></p>

<p class=Text><b><u><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WSS: SOAP
Message Security 1.1 Properties</span></u></b></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Thumbprint
References]</span></b></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
property indicates whether the initiator and recipient MUST be able to process
references using token thumbprints. A value of 'true' indicates that the
initiator and recipient MUST be able to process such references. A value of
'false' indicates that the initiator and recipient MUST NOT generate such
references and that the initiator and recipient MAY send a fault if such
references are encountered.</span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>
This property has a default value of 'false'.</span></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></b></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[EncryptedKey
References]</span></b></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
property indicates whether the initiator and recipient MUST be able to process
references using EncryptedKey references. A value of 'true' indicates that the
initiator and recipient MUST be able to process such references. A value of
'false' indicates that the initiator and recipient MUST NOT generate such
references and that the initiator and recipient MAY send a fault if such
references are encountered.</span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>
This property has a default value of 'false'.</span><b><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'> </span></b></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></b></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Signature
Confirmation]</span></b></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
property specifies whether </span><span style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsse11:SignatureConfirmation</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> elements SHOULD be used as defined in
WSS: Soap Message Security 1.1. If the value is 'true', </span><span
style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsse11:SignatureConfirmation</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> elements MUST be used and signed by
the message signature. If the value is 'false', signature confirmation elements
MUST NOT be used. The value of this property applies to all signatures that are
included in the security header. This property has a default value of 'false'.</span></p>

<h2><a name="_Toc325572770"></a><a name="_Toc182291549"></a><a
name="_Toc161826573"></a><a name="_Toc104103976"></a><a name="_Wss10_Assertion"></a>9.1
Wss10 Assertion</h2>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The Wss10
assertion allows you to specify which WSS: SOAP Message Security 1.0 options
are supported.</span></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:
solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in;
background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;sp:Wss10
xmlns:sp=&quot;...&quot; ... &gt;<br>
  &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp=&quot;...&quot;&gt;<br>
    &lt;sp:MustSupportRefKeyIdentifier  ... /&gt; ?<br>
    &lt;sp:MustSupportRefIssuerSerial  ... /&gt; ?<br>
    &lt;sp:MustSupportRefExternalURI  ... /&gt; ?<br>
    &lt;sp:MustSupportRefEmbeddedToken  ... /&gt; ?<br>
    ...<br>
  &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
  ...<br>
&lt;/sp:Wss10&gt;</p>

</div>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined above:</span></p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'>Wss10</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This identifies a WSS10 assertion.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'>Wss10/wsp:Policy</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This indicates a policy that controls WSS: SOAP Message
Security 1.0 options.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Wss10/wsp:Policy/sp:MustSupportRefKeyIdentifier</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion indicates that
the [Key Identifier References] property is set to 'true'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Wss10/wsp:Policy/sp:MustSupportRefIssuerSerial</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion indicates that
the [Issuer Serial References] property is set to 'true'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Wss10/wsp:Policy/sp:MustSupportRefExternalURI</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion indicates that
the [External URI References] property is set to 'true'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Wss10/wsp:Policy/sp:MustSupportRefEmbeddedToken</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion indicates that
the [Embedded Token References] property is set to 'true'.</p>

<h2><a name="_Toc325572771"></a><a name="_Toc182291550"></a><a
name="_Toc161826574"></a><a name="_Toc104103977"></a><a name="_Wss11_Assertion"></a>9.2
Wss11 Assertion</h2>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The Wss11
assertion allows you to specify which WSS: SOAP Message Security 1.1 options
are supported.</span></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:
solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in;
background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;sp:Wss11
xmlns:sp=&quot;...&quot; ... &gt;<br>
  &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp=&quot;...&quot;&gt;<br>
    &lt;sp:MustSupportRefKeyIdentifier  ... /&gt; ?<br>
    &lt;sp:MustSupportRefIssuerSerial  ... /&gt; ?<br>
    &lt;sp:MustSupportRefExternalURI  ... /&gt; ?<br>
    &lt;sp:MustSupportRefEmbeddedToken  ... /&gt; ?<br>
    &lt;sp:MustSupportRefThumbprint  ... /&gt; ?<br>
    &lt;sp:MustSupportRefEncryptedKey  ... /&gt; ?<br>
    &lt;sp:RequireSignatureConfirmation  ... /&gt; ?<br>
    ...<br>
  &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
&lt;/sp:Wss11&gt;</p>

</div>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined above:</span></p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'>Wss11</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This identifies an WSS11 assertion.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'>Wss11/wsp:Policy</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This indicates a policy that controls WSS: SOAP Message
Security 1.1 options.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Wss11/wsp:Policy/sp:MustSupportRefKeyIdentifier</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion indicates that
the [Key Identifier References] property is set to 'true'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Wss11/wsp:Policy/sp:MustSupportRefIssuerSerial</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion indicates that
the [Issuer Serial References] property is set to 'true'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Wss11/wsp:Policy/sp:MustSupportRefExternalURI</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion indicates that
the [External URI References] property is set to 'true'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Wss11/wsp:Policy/sp:MustSupportRefEmbeddedToken</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion indicates that
the [Embedded Token References] property is set to 'true'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Wss11/wsp:Policy/sp:MustSupportRefThumbprint</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion indicates that
the [Thumbprint References] property is set to 'true'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Wss11/wsp:Policy/sp:MustSupportRefEncryptedKey</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion indicates that
the [EncryptedKey References] property is set to 'true'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Wss11/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireSignatureConfirmation</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion indicates that
the [Signature Confirmation] property is set to 'true'.</p>

<div style='border:none;border-top:solid gray 1.0pt;padding:6.0pt 0in 0in 0in'>

<h1><a name="_Toc85362858"></a><a name="_Toc85350303"></a><a
name="_Toc104103978"></a><a name="_Toc96244205"></a><a name="_Toc95877144"></a><a
name="_Toc95113151"></a><a name="_Toc92518251">10<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>
</span> </a><a name="_Toc325572772"></a><a name="_Toc182291551"></a><a
name="_Toc161826575">WS-Trust Options</a></h1>

</div>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This section
defines the various policy assertions related to exchanges based on WS-Trust,
specifically with client and server challenges and entropy behaviors. These
assertions relate to interactions with a Security Token Service and MAY augment
the behaviors defined by the Binding Property Assertions defined in Section 6.
The assertions defined here MUST apply to [Endpoint Policy Subject].</span></p>

<p class=Text><a name="_Toc96244206"></a><a name="_Toc95877145"></a><a
name="_Toc95113152"></a><a name="_Toc92518252"><b><u><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'><span style='text-decoration:none'>&nbsp;</span></span></u></b></a></p>

<p class=Text><b><u><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WS-Trust 1.3
Properties</span></u></b></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Client
Challenge]</span></b></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
property indicates whether client challenges are supported. A value of 'true'
indicates that </span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>a </span><span
class=CodeChar><span style='font-size:9.0pt'>wst:SignChallenge</span></span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> element is supported inside of an RST
sent by the client to the server.</span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>
A value of 'false' indicates that a </span><span class=CodeChar><span
style='font-size:9.0pt'>wst:SignChallenge</span></span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'> is not supported. </span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>There
is no change in the number of messages exchanged by the client and service in
satisfying the RST. This property has a default value of 'false'.</span></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></b></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Server
Challenge]</span></b></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
property indicates whether server challenges are supported. A value of 'true'
indicates that </span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>a </span><span
class=CodeChar><span style='font-size:9.0pt'>wst:SignChallenge</span></span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> element is supported inside of an
RSTR sent by the server to the client.</span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>
A value of 'false' indicates that a </span><span class=CodeChar><span
style='font-size:9.0pt'>wst:SignChallenge</span></span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'> is not supported. </span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A
challenge issued by the server MAY increase the number of messages exchanged by
the client and service in order to accommodate the </span><span class=CodeChar><span
style='font-size:9.0pt'>wst:SignChallengeResponse</span></span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> element sent by the client to the
server in response to the </span><span class=CodeChar><span style='font-size:
9.0pt'>wst:SignChallenge</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>
element. A final RSTR containing the issued token will follow subsequent to the
server receiving the </span><span class=CodeChar><span style='font-size:9.0pt'>wst:SignChallengeResponse</span></span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> element. This property has a default
value of 'false'.</span></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></b></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Client
Entropy]</span></b></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
property indicates whether client entropy is REQUIRED to be used as key
material for a requested proof token. A value of 'true' indicates that client
entropy is REQUIRED. A value of 'false' indicates that client entropy is NOT
REQUIRED. This property has a default value of 'false'.</span></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></b></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Server
Entropy]</span></b></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
property indicates whether server entropy is REQUIRED to be used as key
material for a requested proof token. A value of 'true' indicates that server
entropy is REQUIRED. A value of 'false' indicates that server entropy is NOT
REQUIRED. This property has a default value of 'false'.</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Note: If both the
[Client Entropy] and [Server Entropy] properties are set to true, Client and
server entropy are combined to produce a computed key using the Computed Key
algorithm defined by the [Algorithm Suite] property. </span></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></b></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Issued Tokens]</span></b></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
property indicates whether the </span><span style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wst:IssuedTokens</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> header is supported as described in
WS-Trust. A value of 'true' indicates that the wst:IssuedTokens header is
supported. A value of 'false' indicates that the </span><span style='font-family:
"Courier New"'>wst:IssuedTokens</span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>
header is not supported. This property has a default value of 'false'.</span></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Collection]</span></b></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
property specifies whether a wst:RequestSecurityTokenCollection element is
present. A value of 'true' indicates that the
wst:RequestSecurityTokenCollection element MUST be present and MUST be
integrity protected either by transport or message level security. A value of
'false' indicates that the wst:RequestSecurityTokenCollection element MUST NOT
be present. This property has a default value of 'false'.</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<h2><a name="_Toc325572773"></a><a name="_Toc182291552"></a><a
name="_Toc161826576"></a><a name="_Toc104103979"></a><a
name="_Trust10_Assertion"></a><a name="_Trust130_Assertion"></a>10.1 Trust13
Assertion</h2>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The Trust13
assertion allows you to specify which WS-Trust 1.3 options are supported.</span></p>

<p class=Text><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:
solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in;
background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;sp:Trust13
xmlns:sp=&quot;...&quot; ... &gt;<br>
  &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp=&quot;...&quot;&gt;<br>
    &lt;sp:MustSupportClientChallenge  ... /&gt;?<br>
    &lt;sp:MustSupportServerChallenge  ... /&gt;?<br>
    &lt;sp:RequireClientEntropy  ... /&gt;?<br>
    &lt;sp:RequireServerEntropy  ... /&gt;?<br>
    &lt;sp:MustSupportIssuedTokens  ... /&gt;?</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>   
&lt;sp:RequireRequestSecurityTokenCollection /&gt;?</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>   
&lt;sp:RequireAppliesTo /&gt;?<br>
    ...<br>
  &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
  ...<br>
&lt;/sp:Trust13 ... &gt;</p>

</div>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined above:</span></p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'>Trust13</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This identifies a Trust13 assertion.</p>

<p class=DefinedTerm><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'>Trust13/wsp:Policy</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This indicates a policy that controls WS-Trust 1.3 options.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Trust13/wsp:Policy/sp:MustSupportClientChallenge</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion indicates that
the [Client Challenge] property is set to 'true'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Trust13/wsp:Policy/sp:MustSupportServerChallenge</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion indicates that
the [Server Challenge] property is set to 'true'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Trust13/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireClientEntropy</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion indicates that
the [Client Entropy] property is set to 'true'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Trust13/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireServerEntropy</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion indicates that
the [Server Entropy] property is set to 'true'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Trust13/wsp:Policy/sp:MustSupportIssuedTokens</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion indicates that
the [Issued Tokens] property is set to 'true'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Trust13/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireRequestSecurityTokenCollection</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the [Collection] property is set to 'true'.</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Trust13/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireAppliesTo</span></p>

<p class=Definition>This OPTIONAL element is a policy assertion that indicates
that the STS requires the requestor to specify the scope for the issued token
using wsp:AppliesTo in the RST.</p>

<div style='border:none;border-top:solid gray 1.0pt;padding:6.0pt 0in 0in 0in'>

<h1><a name="_Toc104103980"></a><a name="_Toc96244213"></a><a
name="_Toc95877152"></a><a name="_Toc95113159"></a><a name="_Toc325572774"></a><a
name="_Toc182291553"></a><a name="_Toc161826577">11<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>
</span>Guidance on creating new assertions and assertion extensibility</a></h1>

</div>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This non-normative
appendix provides guidance for designers of new assertions intended for use
with this specification.</span></p>

<h2><a name="_Toc325572775"></a><a name="_Toc182291554"></a><a
name="_Toc161826578">11.1 General Design Points</a></h2>
