<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:Symbol'>·<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Prefer Distinct
Qnames</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:Symbol'>·<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Parameterize using
nested policy where possible.</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:Symbol'>·<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Parameterize using
attributes and/or child elements where necessary.</span></p>

<h2><a name="_Toc325572776"></a><a name="_Toc182291555"></a><a
name="_Toc161826579">11.2 Detailed Design Guidance</a></h2>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Assertions in
WS-SP are XML elements that are identified by their QName. Matching of
assertions per WS-Policy is performed by matching element QNames. Matching does
not take into account attributes that are present on the assertion element. Nor
does it take into account child elements except for wsp:Policy elements. If a
wsp:Policy element is present, then matching occurs against the assertions
nested inside that wsp:Policy element recursively (see </span><a
href="http://www.w3.org/Submission/WS-Policy/#Policy_Assertion_Nesting"><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Policy Assertion Nesting</span></a><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> [</span><a href="#wspolicy"><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WS-Policy</span></a><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>]).</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>When designing new
assertions for use with WS-SP, the above matching behaviour needs to be taken
into account. In general, multiple assertions with distinct QNames are
preferably to a single assertion that uses attributes and/or content to
distinguish different cases. For example, given two possible assertion designs;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Example>Design 1<br>
<br>
   &lt;A1/&gt;<br>
   &lt;A2/&gt;<br>
   &lt;A3/&gt;<br>
<br>
Design 2.<br>
<br>
   &lt;A Parameter='1' /&gt;<br>
   &lt;A Parameter='2' /&gt;<br>
   &lt;A Parameter='3' /&gt;<br>
<br>
</p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>then design 1.
would generally be prefered because it allows the policy matching logic to
provide more accurate matches between policies.</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A good example of
design 1 is the token assertions defined in Section 5. The section defines 10
distinct token assertions, rather than a single sp:Token assertion with, for
example, a TokenType attribute. These distinct token assertions make policy
matching much more useful as less false positives are generated when performing
policy matching.</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>There are cases
where using attributes or child elements as parameters in assertion design is
reasonable. Examples include cases when implementations are expected to
understand all the values for a given parameter and when encoding the parameter
information into the assertion QName would result in an unmanageable number of
assertions. A good example is the sp:IncludeToken attribute that appears on the
various token assertions. Five possible values are currently specified for the
sp:IncludeToken attribute and implementations are expected to understand the
meaning of all 5 values. If this information was encoded into the assertion
QNames, each existing token assertion would require five variants, one for each
Uri value which would result in 45 assertions just for the tokens defined in
Section 5.</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Nested policy is
ideal for encoding parameters that can be usefully matched using policy
matching. For example, the token version assertions defined in Section 5 use
such an approach. The overall token type assertion is parameterized by the
nested token version assertions. Policy matching can use these parameters to
find matches between policies where the broad token type is support by both
parties but they might not support the same specific versions.</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Note, when
designing assertions for new token types such assertions SHOULD allow the
sp:IncludeToken attribute and SHOULD allow nested policy.</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<div style='border:none;border-top:solid gray 1.0pt;padding:6.0pt 0in 0in 0in'>

<h1><a name="_Toc325572777"></a><a name="_Toc182291556"></a><a
name="_Toc161826580">12<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'> </span>Security
Considerations</a> </h1>

</div>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>It is strongly
recommended that policies and assertions be signed to prevent tampering.  </span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>It is recommended
that policies should not be accepted unless they are signed and have an
associated security token to specify the signer has proper claims for the given
policy.  That is, a party shouldn't rely on a policy unless the policy is
signed and presented with sufficient claims. It is further recommended that the
entire policy exchange mechanism be protected to prevent man-in-the-middle
downgrade attacks.</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>It should be noted
that the mechanisms described in this document could be secured as part of a
SOAP message using WSS: SOAP Message Security [<a href="#wss10">WSS10</a>, <a
href="#wss11">WSS11</a>] or embedded within other objects using object-specific
security mechanisms.</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>It is recommended
that policies not specify two (or more) SignedSupportingTokens or
SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens of the same token type. Messages conforming to
such policies are subject to modification which may be undetectable.</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>It is recommended
that policies specify the OnlySignEntireHeadersAndBody assertion along with the
rest of the policy in order to combat certain XML substitution attacks.</span></p>

<div style='border:none;border-top:solid gray 1.0pt;padding:6.0pt 0in 0in 0in'>

<p class=AppendixHeading1 style='margin-left:.3in;text-indent:-.3in'><a
name=AppendixA></a><a name="_Toc325572778"></a><a name="_Toc182291557"></a><a
name="_Toc161826581"></a><a name="_Toc104103983"></a><a name="_Toc100567689">Appendix
A.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'> </span>Assertions and
WS-PolicyAttachment</a></p>

</div>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This non-normative
appendix classifies assertions according to their suggested scope in WSDL 1.1
per Section 4 of [WS-PolicyAttachment]. See Figure 1 in Section 4.1 of
[WS-PolicyAttachment] for a graphical representation of the relationship
between policy scope and WSDL. Unless otherwise noted above, any assertion that
is listed under multiple [Policy Subjects] below MUST only apply to only one
[Policy Subject] in a WSDL 1.1 hierarchy for calculating an Effective Policy.</span></p>

<p class=AppendixHeading2><a name="_Toc325572779"></a><a name="_Toc182291558"></a><a

<p class=MsoNormal><a name="_Toc100567704">The assertions listed in this
section do not have a defined policy subject because they appear nested inside
some other assertion which does have a defined policy subject. This list is
derived from nested assertions in the specification that have independent
sections. It is not a complete list of nested assertions. Many of the
assertions previously listed in this appendix as well as the ones below have
additional nested assertions.</a></p>

<p class=AppendixHeading3><a name="_Toc325572792"></a><a name="_Toc182291571"></a><a

name="_Toc161826596"></a><a name="_Toc104103997">A.4.2 Token Usage Assertions</a></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>See the nested
assertions under the </span><a href="#_TransportBinding_Assertion"><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>TransportBinding</span></a><span

<p class=AppendixHeading3><a name="_Toc325572794"></a><a name="_Toc182291573"></a><a
name="_Toc161826597"></a><a name="_Toc104103998">A.4.3 Token Assertions</a></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><a
href="#_UsernameToken_Assertion">UsernameToken Assertion</a>                                                         (Section
5.3.1)</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><a
href="#_IssuedToken_Assertion">IssuedToken Assertion</a>                                                              (Section
5.3.2)</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><a
href="#_X509Token_Assertion">X509Token Assertion</a>                                                                 (Section
5.3.3)</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><a
href="#_KerberosToken_Assertion">KerberosToken Assertion</a>                                                           (Section
5.3.4)</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><a
href="#_SpnegoContextToken_Assertion">SpnegoContextToken Assertion</a>                                                 (Section
5.3.5)</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><a
href="#_SecurityContextToken_Assertion">SecurityContextToken Assertion</a>                                                 (Section
5.3.6)</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><a
href="#_SecureConversationToken_Assertion">SecureConversationToken Assertion</a>                                          (Section
5.3.7)</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><a
href="#_SamlToken_Assertion">SamlToken Assertion</a>                                                                 (Section
5.3.8)</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><a
href="#_RelToken_Assertion">RelToken Assertion</a>                                                                   (Section
5.3.9)</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><a
href="#_HttpsToken_Assertion">HttpsToken Assertion</a>                                                                (Section
5.3.10)</span></p>

<div style='border:none;border-top:solid gray 1.0pt;padding:6.0pt 0in 0in 0in'>

<p class=AppendixHeading1 style='margin-left:.3in;text-indent:-.3in'><a
name=AppendixB></a><a name="_Toc325572795"></a><a name="_Toc182291574"></a><a
name="_Toc161826598"></a><a name="_Toc104104002">Appendix B.<span
style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'> </span>Issued Token Policy</a></p>

</div>

<p class=MsoNormal>The section provides further detail about behavior
associated with the IssuedToken assertion in section 5.3.2. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal>The issued token security model involves a three-party
setup. There’s a target Server, a Client, and a trusted third party called a
Security Token Service or STS. Policy flows from Server to Client, and from STS
to Client. Policy MAY be embedded inside an Issued Token assertion, or acquired
out-of-band. There MAY be an explicit trust relationship between the Server and
the STS. There MUST be a trust relationship between the Client and the STS.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal>The Issued Token policy assertion includes two parts: 1)
client-specific parameters that MUST be understood and processed by the client
and 2) STS specific parameters which are to be processed by the STS. The format
of the Issued Token policy assertion is illustrated in the figure below.</p>

<p class=Figure><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><img border=0
width=150 height=131
src="ws-securitypolicy-1.2-errata01-os-complete_files/image012.gif"></span></p>

<p class=MsoNormal>The client-specific parameters of the Issued Token policy
assertion along with the remainder of the server policy are consumed by the
client. The STS specific parameters of the Issued Token policy assertion are
passed on to the STS by copying the parameters directly into the <span
style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wst:SecondaryParameters</span> of the RST
request sent by the Client to the STS as illustrated in the figure below. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>

<p class=Figure><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><img border=0
width=399 height=214
src="ws-securitypolicy-1.2-errata01-os-complete_files/image013.gif"></span></p>

<p class=MsoNormal>Before the Client sends the RST to the STS, it will need to
obtain the policy for the STS. This will help to formulate the RST request and
will include any security-specific requirements of the STS. </p>

<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal>The Client MAY augment or replace the contents of the RST
made to the STS based on the Client-specific parameters received from the
Issued Token policy assertion contained in the Server policy, from policy it
received for the STS, or any other local parameters.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal>The Issued Token Policy Assertion contains elements which MUST
be understood by the Client. The assertion contains one element which contains
a list of arbitrary elements which SHOULD be sent along to the STS by copying
the elements as-is directly into the <span style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wst:SecondaryParameters</span>
of the RST request sent by the Client to the STS following the protocol defined
in WS-Trust.</p>

<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>

<p class=MsoNormal>Elements inside the <span style='font-family:"Courier New"'>sp:RequestSecurityTokenTemplate</span>
element MUST conform to WS-Trust [<a href="#wstrust">WS-Trust</a>]. All items
are OPTIONAL, since the Server and STS may already have a pre-arranged
relationship which specifies some or all of the conditions and constraints for
issued tokens.</p>

<div style='border:none;border-top:solid gray 1.0pt;padding:6.0pt 0in 0in 0in'>

<p class=AppendixHeading1 style='margin-left:.3in;text-indent:-.3in'><a
name="_Toc325572796"></a><a name="_Toc182291575"></a><a name="_Toc161826599"></a><a
name="_Toc104104003"></a><a name="_Toc100567706"></a><a name=AppendixC></a>Appendix
C.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'> </span>Strict Security Header
Layout Examples</p>

</div>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
sections describe the security header layout for specific bindings when
applying the ‘Strict’ layout rules defined in Section 6.7.</span></p>

<p class=AppendixHeading2><a name="_Toc325572797"></a><a name="_Toc182291576"></a><a
name="_Toc161826600"></a><a name="_Toc104104004"></a><a name="_Toc100567707">C.1
Transport Binding</a></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This section
describes how the ‘Strict’ security header layout rules apply to the Transport
Binding.</span></p>

<p class=AppendixHeading3><a name="_Toc325572798"></a><a name="_Toc182291577"></a><a
name="_Toc161826601"></a><a name="_Toc104104005"></a><a name="_Toc100567708">C.1.1
Policy</a></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
example shows a policy indicating a Transport Binding, an Https Token as the
Transport Token, an algorithm suite, a requirement to include tokens in the
supporting signatures, a username token attached to the message, and finally an
X509 token attached to the message and endorsing the message signature. No
message protection requirements are described since the transport covers all
message parts.</span></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:
solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in;
background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;wsp:Policy
xmlns:wsp=&quot;...&quot; xmlns:sp=&quot;...&quot;&gt;<br>
  &lt;sp:TransportBinding&gt;<br>
    &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
      &lt;sp:TransportToken&gt;<br>
        &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
          &lt;sp:HttpsToken /&gt;<br>
        &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
      &lt;/sp:TransportToken&gt;<br>
      &lt;sp:AlgorithmSuite&gt;<br>
        &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
          &lt;sp:Basic256 /&gt;<br>
        &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
      &lt;/sp:AlgorithmSuite&gt;<br>
      &lt;sp:Layout&gt;<br>
        &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
          &lt;sp:Strict /&gt;<br>
        &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
      &lt;/sp:Layout&gt;<br>
      &lt;sp:IncludeTimestamp /&gt;<br>
    &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
  &lt;/sp:TransportBinding&gt;<br>
  &lt;sp:SignedSupportingTokens&gt;<br>
    &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
      &lt;sp:UsernameToken sp:IncludeToken=&quot;.../IncludeToken/Once&quot;
/&gt;<br>
    &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
  &lt;/sp:SignedSupportingTokens&gt;<br>
  &lt;sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens&gt;<br>
    &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
      &lt;sp:X509Token sp:IncludeToken=&quot;.../IncludeToken/Once&quot;&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>       
&lt;wsp:Policy&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>         
&lt;sp:WssX509v3Token10 /&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>       
&lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>     
&lt;/sp:X509Token&gt;<br>
    &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
  &lt;/sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens&gt;<br>
  &lt;sp:Wss11&gt;<br>
    &lt;sp:RequireSignatureConfirmation /&gt;<br>
  &lt;/sp:Wss11&gt;<br>
&lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;</p>

</div>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This policy is
used as the basis for the examples shown in the subsequent section describing
the security header layout for this binding.</span></p>

<p class=AppendixHeading3><a name="_Toc325572799"></a><a name="_Toc182291578"></a><a
name="_Toc161826602"></a><a name="_Toc104104006"></a><a name="_Toc100567709">C.1.2
Initiator to Recipient Messages</a></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Messages sent from
initiator to recipient have the following layout for the security header:</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>1.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A </span><span
style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsu:Timestamp</span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'> element.</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>2.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Any tokens
contained in the [Signed Supporting Tokens] property.</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>3.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Any tokens
contained in the [Signed Endorsing Supporting Tokens] property each followed by
the corresponding signature. Each signature MUST cover the </span><span
class=CodeEmbedded>wsu:Timestamp</span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>
element from 1 above and SHOULD cover any other unique identifier for the
message in order to prevent replays. If [Token Protection] is 'true', the
signature MUST also cover the supporting token. If [Derived Keys] is 'true' and
the supporting token is associated with a symmetric key, then a Derived Key
Token, based on the supporting token, appears between the supporting token and
the signature.</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>4.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Any signatures for
tokens contained in the [Endorsing Supporting Tokens] property. Each signature
MUST cover the </span><span class=CodeEmbedded>wsu:Timestamp</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> element from 1 above and SHOULD cover
at least some other unique identifier for the message in order to prevent
replays. If [Token Protection] is 'true', the signature MUST also cover the
supporting token. If [Derived Keys] is 'true' and the supporting token is
associated with a symmetric key, then a Derived Key Token, based on the
supporting token, appears before the signature.</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
diagram illustrates the security header layout for the initiator to recipient
message:</span></p>

<p class=Figure><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><img border=0
width=346 height=512
src="ws-securitypolicy-1.2-errata01-os-complete_files/image014.gif"></span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The outer box
shows that the entire message is protected (signed and encrypted) by the
transport. The arrows on the left from the box labeled Sig<sub>2</sub> indicate
the parts signed by the supporting token labeled ST<sub>2</sub>, namely the
message timestamp labeled TS and the token used as the basis for the signature
labeled ST<sub>2</sub>. The dotted arrow indicates the token that was used as
the basis for the signature. In general, the ordering of the items in the
security header follows the most optimal layout for a receiver to process its
contents.</span></p>

<p class=Text><i><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Example:</span></i></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Initiator to
recipient message</span></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:
solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in;
background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;S:Envelope
xmlns:S=&quot;...&quot; xmlns:wsse=&quot;...&quot; xmlns:wsu=&quot;...&quot;
xmlns:ds=&quot;...&quot;&gt;<br>
  &lt;S:Header&gt;<br>
    ...<br>
    &lt;wsse:Security&gt;<br>
      &lt;wsu:Timestamp wsu:Id=&quot;timestamp&quot;&gt;<br>
        &lt;wsu:Created&gt;[datetime]&lt;/wsu:Created&gt;<br>
        &lt;wsu:Expires&gt;[datetime]&lt;/wsu:Expires&gt;<br>
      &lt;/wsu:Timestamp&gt;<br>
      &lt;wsse:UsernameToken wsu:Id='SomeSignedToken' &gt;<br>
      ...<br>
      &lt;/wsse:UsernameToken&gt;<br>
      &lt;wsse:BinarySecurityToken wsu:Id=&quot;SomeSignedEndorsingToken&quot;
&gt;<br>
      ...<br>
      &lt;/wsse:BinarySecurityToken&gt;<br>
      &lt;ds:Signature&gt;<br>
        &lt;ds:SignedInfo&gt;<br>
          &lt;ds:References&gt;<br>
            &lt;ds:Reference URI=&quot;#timestamp&quot; /&gt;<br>
            &lt;ds:Reference URI=&quot;#SomeSignedEndorsingToken&quot; /&gt;<br>
          &lt;/ds:References&gt;<br>
        &lt;/ds:SignedInfo&gt;<br>
        &lt;ds:SignatureValue&gt;...&lt;/ds:SignatureValue&gt;<br>
        &lt;ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br>
          &lt;wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br>
            &lt;wsse:Reference URI=&quot;#SomeSignedEndorsingToken&quot; /&gt;<br>
          &lt;/wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br>
        &lt;/ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br>
      &lt;/ds:Signature&gt;<br>
      ...<br>
    &lt;/wsse:Security&gt;<br>
    ...<br>
  &lt;/S:Header&gt;<br>
  &lt;S:Body&gt;<br>
    ...<br>
  &lt;/S:Body&gt;<br>
&lt;/S:Envelope&gt;</p>

</div>

<p class=AppendixHeading3><a name="_Toc325572800"></a><a name="_Toc182291579"></a><a
name="_Toc161826603"></a><a name="_Toc104104007"></a><a name="_Toc100567710">C.1.3
Recipient to Initiator Messages</a></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Messages sent from
recipient to initiator have the following layout for the security header:</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>1.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A </span><span
style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsu:Timestamp</span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'> element.</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>2.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If the [Signature
Confirmation] property has a value of 'true', then a </span><span
style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsse11:SignatureConfirmation</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> element for each signature in the
corresponding message sent from initiator to recipient. If there are no
signatures in the corresponding message from the initiator to the recipient,
then a </span><span style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsse11:SignatureConfirmation</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> element with no Value attribute.</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
diagram illustrates the security header layout for the recipient to initiator
message:</span></p>

<p class=Figure><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><img border=0
width=347 height=434
src="ws-securitypolicy-1.2-errata01-os-complete_files/image015.gif"></span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The outer box
shows that the entire message is protected (signed and encrypted) by the transport.
One </span><span style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsse11:SignatureConfirmation</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> element labeled SC<sub>1</sub>
corresponding to the signature in the initial message illustrated previously is
included. In general, the ordering of the items in the security header follows
the most optimal layout for a receiver to process its contents.</span></p>

<p class=Text><i><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Example:</span></i></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Recipient to
initiator message</span></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:
solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in;
background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;S:Envelope
xmlns:S=&quot;...&quot; xmlns:wsse=&quot;...&quot; xmlns:wsu=&quot;...&quot;
xmlns:wsse11=&quot;...&quot;&gt;<br>
  &lt;S:Header&gt;<br>
    ...<br>
    &lt;wsse:Security&gt;<br>
      &lt;wsu:Timestamp wsu:Id=&quot;timestamp&quot;&gt;<br>
        &lt;wsu:Created&gt;[datetime]&lt;/wsu:Created&gt;<br>
        &lt;wsu:Expires&gt;[datetime]&lt;/wsu:Expires&gt;<br>
      &lt;/wsu:Timestamp&gt;<br>
      &lt;wsse11:SignatureConfirmation Value=&quot;...&quot; /&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>     
...<br>
    &lt;/wsse:Security&gt;<br>
    ...<br>
  &lt;/S:Header&gt;<br>
  &lt;S:Body&gt;<br>
    ...<br>
  &lt;/S:Body&gt;<br>
&lt;/S:Envelope&gt;</p>

</div>

<p class=AppendixHeading2><a name="_Toc325572801"></a><a name="_Toc182291580"></a><a
name="_Toc161826604"></a><a name="_Toc104104008"></a><a name="_Toc100567711">C.2
Symmetric Binding</a></p>

<p class=Text><a name="_Toc99269308"></a><a name="_Toc96244173"></a><a
name="_Toc95877119"></a><a name="_Toc95113124"></a><a name="_Toc85362812"></a><a
name="_Toc85350257"></a><a name="_Toc79810730"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
section describes how the ‘Strict’ security header layout rules apply to the
Symmetric Binding.</span></a></p>

<p class=AppendixHeading3><a name="_Toc325572802"></a><a name="_Toc182291581"></a><a
name="_Toc161826605"></a><a name="_Toc104104009"></a><a name="_Toc100567712">C.2.1
Policy</a></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
example shows a policy indicating a Symmetric Binding, a symmetric key based
IssuedToken provided as the Protection <a name="_Toc85362815"></a><a
name="_Toc85350260"></a><a name="_Toc79810733">Token, an algorithm suite, a
requirement to encrypt the message parts before signing, a requirement to
encrypt the message signature, a requirement to include tokens in the message
signature and the supporting signatures, a username token attached to the
message, and finally an X509 token attached to the message and endorsing the
message signature. Minimum message protection requirements are described as
well.</a></span></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:
solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in;
background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;!--
Example Endpoint Policy --&gt;<br>
&lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp=&quot;...&quot; xmlns:sp=&quot;...&quot;&gt;<br>
  &lt;sp:SymmetricBinding&gt;<br>
    &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
      &lt;sp:ProtectionToken&gt;<br>
        &lt;sp:IssuedToken sp:IncludeToken=&quot;.../IncludeToken/Once&quot;
&gt;<br>
          &lt;sp:Issuer&gt;...&lt;/sp:Issuer&gt;<br>
          &lt;sp:RequestSecurityTokenTemplate&gt;<br>
          ...<br>
          &lt;/sp:RequestSecurityTokenTemplate&gt;<br>
        &lt;/sp:IssuedToken&gt;<br>
      &lt;/sp:ProtectionToken&gt;<br>
      &lt;sp:AlgorithmSuite&gt;<br>
        &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
          &lt;sp:Basic256 /&gt;<br>
        &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
      &lt;/sp:AlgorithmSuite&gt;<br>
      &lt;sp:Layout&gt;<br>
        &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
          &lt;sp:Strict /&gt;<br>
        &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
      &lt;/sp:Layout&gt;<br>
      &lt;sp:IncludeTimestamp /&gt;<br>
      &lt;sp:EncryptBeforeSigning /&gt;<br>
      &lt;sp:EncryptSignature /&gt;<br>
      &lt;sp:ProtectTokens /&gt;<br>
      &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
  &lt;/sp:SymmetricBinding&gt;<br>
  &lt;sp:SignedSupportingTokens&gt;<br>
    &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
      &lt;sp:UsernameToken sp:IncludeToken=&quot;.../IncludeToken/Once&quot;
/&gt;<br>
    &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
  &lt;/sp:SignedSupportingTokens&gt;<br>
  &lt;sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens&gt;<br>
    &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
      &lt;sp:X509Token sp:IncludeToken=&quot;.../IncludeToken/Once&quot;&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>       
&lt;wsp:Policy&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>         
&lt;sp:WssX509v3Token10 /&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>       
&lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>     
&lt;/sp:X509Token&gt;<br>
    &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
  &lt;/sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens&gt;<br>
  &lt;sp:Wss11&gt;<br>
    &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
      &lt;sp:RequireSignatureConfirmation /&gt;<br>
    &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
  &lt;/sp:Wss11&gt;<br>
&lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
<br>
</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><br>
&lt;!-- Example Message Policy --&gt;<br>
&lt;wsp:Policy<span style='color:blue'> </span>xmlns:wsp=&quot;...&quot;
xmlns:sp=&quot;...&quot;&gt;<br>
  &lt;sp:SignedParts&gt;<br>
    &lt;sp:Header Name=&quot;Header1&quot; Namespace=&quot;...&quot; /&gt;<br>
    &lt;sp:Header Name=&quot;Header2&quot; Namespace=&quot;...&quot; /&gt;<br>
    &lt;sp:Body/&gt;<br>
  &lt;/sp:SignedParts&gt;<br>
  &lt;sp:EncryptedParts&gt;<br>
    &lt;sp:Header Name=&quot;Header2&quot; Namespace=&quot;...&quot; /&gt;<br>
    &lt;sp:Body/&gt;<br>
  &lt;/sp:EncryptedParts&gt;<br>
&lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;</p>

</div>

<p class=Text><a name="_Toc96244176"></a><a name="_Toc95877122"></a><a
name="_Toc95113127"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This policy
is used as the basis for the examples shown in the subsequent section
describing the security header layout for this binding.</span></a></p>

<p class=AppendixHeading3><a name="_Toc325572803"></a><a name="_Toc182291582"></a><a
name="_Toc161826606"></a><a name="_Toc104104010"></a><a name="_Toc100567713">C.2.2
Initiator to Recipient Messages</a></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Messages sent from
initiator to recipient have the following layout for the security header:</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>1.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A </span><span
style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsu:Timestamp</span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'> element if [Timestamp] is 'true'.</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>2.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If the </span><span
style='font-family:"Courier New"'>sp:IncludeToken</span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'> attribute on the [Encryption Token] is
.../IncludeToken/Once or .../IncludeToken/Always, then the [Encryption Token].</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>3.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If [Derived Keys]
is 'true', then a Derived Key Token, based on the [Encryption Token]. This
Derived Key Token is used for encryption.</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>4.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A reference list
including references to encrypted items. If [Signature Protection] is 'true',
then the reference list MUST include a reference to the message signature. If
[Protection Order] is 'SignBeforeEncrypting', then the reference list MUST
include a reference to all the message parts specified in the EncryptedParts
assertions in the policy. If [Derived Keys] is 'true', then the key in the
token from 3 above MUST be used, otherwise the key in the [Encryption Token].</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>5.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Any tokens from
the [Signed Supporting Tokens] and [Signed Endorsing Supporting Tokens]
properties whose </span><span style='font-family:"Courier New"'>sp:IncludeToken
</span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>attribute is
.../IncludeToken/Once or .../IncludeToken/Always.</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>6.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If the [Signature
Token] is not the same as the [Encryption Token], and the </span><span
style='font-family:"Courier New"'>sp:IncludeToken</span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'> attribute on the [Signature Token] is
.../IncludeToken/Once or .../IncludeToken/Always, then the [Signature Token].</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>7.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If [Derived Keys]
is 'true', then a Derived Key Token based on the [Signature Token]. This
Derived Key Token is used for signature.</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>8.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A signature over
the </span><span style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsu:Timestamp</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> from 1 above, any tokens from 5 above
regardless of whether they are included in the message, and any message parts
specified in SignedParts assertions in the policy. If [Token Protection] is
'true', the signature MUST cover the [Signature Token] regardless of whether it
is included in the message. If [Derived Keys] is 'true', the key in the token
from 7 above MUST be used, otherwise the key in the [Signature Token] from 6
above.</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>9.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Signatures
covering the main signature from 8 above for any tokens from the [Endorsing
Supporting Tokens] and [Signed Endorsing Supporting Tokens] properties. If
[Token Protection] is 'true', the signature MUST also cover the endorsing
token. If [Derived Keys] is 'true' and the endorsing token is associated with a
symmetric key, then a Derived Key Token, based on the endorsing token, appears
before the signature.</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>10.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If [Protection
Order] is 'EncryptBeforeSigning', then a reference list referencing all the
message parts specified in EncryptedParts assertions in the policy. If [Derived
Keys] is 'true', then the key in the token from 3 above MUST be used, otherwise
the key in the [Encryption Token] from 2 above.</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
diagram illustrates the security header layout for the initiator to recipient
message:</span></p>

<p class=Figure><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><img border=0
width=575 height=627
src="ws-securitypolicy-1.2-errata01-os-complete_files/image016.gif"></span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The arrows on the
right indicate parts that were signed as part of the message signature labeled
Sig<sub>1</sub>. The dashed arrows on the left from the box labeled Sig<sub>2</sub>
indicate the parts signed by the supporting token labeled ST<sub>2</sub>,
namely the message signature labeled Sig<sub>1</sub> and the token used as the
basis for the signature labeled ST<sub>2</sub>. The arrows on the left from
boxes labeled Ref<sub>1</sub> indicate references to parts encrypted using a
key based on the Shared Secret Token labeled ST<sub>1</sub>. The dotted arrows
inside the box labeled Security indicate the token that was used as the basis
for each cryptographic operation. In general, the ordering of the items in the
security header follows the most optimal layout for a receiver to process its
contents.</span></p>

<p class=Text><i><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Example:</span></i></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Initiator to
recipient message using EncryptBeforeSigning:</span></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:
solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in;
background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;S:Envelope
xmlns:S=&quot;...&quot; xmlns:x=&quot;...&quot; xmlns:wsu=&quot;...&quot; </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  xmlns:wsse11=&quot;...&quot;
xmlns:wsse=&quot;...&quot; xmlns:saml=&quot;...&quot; </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'> 
xmlns:xenc=&quot;...&quot; xmlns:ds=&quot;...&quot;&gt;<br>
  &lt;S:Header&gt;<br>
    &lt;x:Header1 wsu:Id=&quot;Header1&quot; &gt;<br>
    ...<br>
    &lt;/x:Header1&gt;<br>
<br>
</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>   
&lt;wsse11:EncryptedHeader wsu:Id=&quot;enc_Header2&quot;&gt;<br>
      &lt;!-- Plaintext Header2<br>
      &lt;x:Header2 wsu:Id=&quot;Header2&quot; &gt;<br>
      ...<br>
      &lt;/x:Header2&gt;<br>
      --&gt;<br>
      ...<br>
    &lt;/wsse11:EncryptedHeader&gt;<br>
    ...<br>
    &lt;wsse:Security&gt;<br>
      &lt;wsu:Timestamp wsu:Id=&quot;Timestamp&quot;&gt;<br>
        &lt;wsu:Created&gt;...&lt;/wsu:Created&gt;<br>
        &lt;wsu:Expires&gt;...&lt;/wsu:Expires&gt;<br>
      &lt;/wsu:Timestamp&gt;<br>
      &lt;saml:Assertion AssertionId=&quot;_SharedSecretToken&quot; ...&gt;<br>
      ...<br>
      &lt;/saml:Assertion&gt;<br>
      &lt;xenc:ReferenceList&gt;<br>
        &lt;xenc:DataReference URI=&quot;#enc_Signature&quot; /&gt;<br>
        &lt;xenc:DataReference URI=&quot;#enc_SomeUsernameToken&quot; /&gt;<br>
        ...<br>
      &lt;/xenc:ReferenceList&gt;<br>
      &lt;xenc:EncryptedData ID=&quot;enc_SomeUsernameToken&quot; &gt;<br>
        &lt;!-- Plaintext UsernameToken<br>
        &lt;wsse:UsernameToken wsu:Id=&quot;SomeUsernameToken&quot; &gt;<br>
        ...<br>
        &lt;/wsse:UsernameToken&gt; <br>
        --&gt;<br>
        ...<br>
        &lt;ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br>
          &lt;wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br>
            &lt;wsse:Reference URI=&quot;#_SharedSecretToken&quot; /&gt;<br>
          &lt;/wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br>
        &lt;/ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br>
      &lt;/xenc:EncryptedData&gt;<br>
      &lt;wsse:BinarySecurityToken wsu:Id=&quot;SomeSupportingToken&quot; &gt;<br>
      ...<br>
      &lt;/wsse:BinarySecurityToken&gt;<br>
      &lt;xenc:EncryptedData ID=&quot;enc_Signature&quot;&gt;<br>
        &lt;!-- Plaintext Signature<br>
        &lt;ds:Signature Id=&quot;Signature&quot;&gt;<br>
          &lt;ds:SignedInfo&gt;<br>
            &lt;ds:References&gt;<br>
              &lt;ds:Reference URI=&quot;#Timestamp&quot; &gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br>
              &lt;ds:Reference URI=&quot;#SomeUsernameToken&quot;
&gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br>
              &lt;ds:Reference URI=&quot;#SomeSupportingToken&quot;
&gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br>
              &lt;ds:Reference URI=&quot;#_SharedSecretToken&quot;
&gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br>
              &lt;ds:Reference URI=&quot;#Header1&quot;
&gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br>
              &lt;ds:Reference URI=&quot;#Header2&quot;
&gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br>
              &lt;ds:Reference URI=&quot;#Body&quot;
&gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br>
            &lt;/ds:References&gt;<br>
          &lt;/ds:SignedInfo&gt;<br>
          &lt;ds:SignatureValue&gt;...&lt;/ds:SignatureValue&gt;<br>
          &lt;ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br>
            &lt;wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br>
              &lt;wsse:Reference URI=&quot;#_SharedSecretToken&quot; /&gt;<br>
            &lt;/wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br>
          &lt;/ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br>
        &lt;/ds:Signature&gt;<br>
        --&gt;<br>
        ...<br>
        &lt;ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br>
          &lt;wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br>
            &lt;wsse:Reference URI=&quot;#_SharedSecretToken&quot; /&gt;<br>
          &lt;/wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br>
        &lt;/ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br>
      &lt;/xenc:EncryptedData&gt;<br>
      &lt;ds:Signature&gt;<br>
        &lt;ds:SignedInfo&gt;<br>
          &lt;ds:References&gt;<br>
            &lt;ds:Reference URI=&quot;#Signature&quot;
&gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br>
            &lt;ds:Reference URI=&quot;#SomeSupportingToken&quot;
&gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br>
          &lt;/ds:References&gt;<br>
        &lt;/ds:SignedInfo&gt;<br>
        &lt;ds:SignatureValue&gt;...&lt;/ds:SignatureValue&gt;<br>
        &lt;ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br>
          &lt;wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br>
            &lt;wsse:Reference URI=&quot;#SomeSupportingToken&quot; /&gt;<br>
          &lt;/wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br>
        &lt;/ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br>
      &lt;/ds:Signature&gt;<br>
      &lt;xenc:ReferenceList&gt;<br>
        &lt;xenc:DataReference URI=&quot;#enc_Body&quot; /&gt;<br>
        &lt;xenc:DataReference URI=&quot;#enc_Header2&quot; /&gt;<br>
        ...<br>
      &lt;/xenc:ReferenceList&gt;<br>
    &lt;/wsse:Security&gt;<br>
  &lt;/S:Header&gt;<br>
  &lt;S:Body wsu:Id=&quot;Body&quot;&gt;<br>
    &lt;xenc:EncryptedData Id=&quot;enc_Body&quot;&gt;<br>
      ...<br>
      &lt;ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br>
        &lt;wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br>
          &lt;wsse:Reference URI=&quot;#_SharedSecretToken&quot; /&gt;<br>
        &lt;/wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br>
      &lt;/ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br>
    &lt;/xenc:EncryptedData&gt;<br>
  &lt;/S:Body&gt;<br>
&lt;/S:Envelope&gt;</p>

</div>

<p class=AppendixHeading3><a name="_Toc325572804"></a><a name="_Toc182291583"></a><a
name="_Toc161826607"></a><a name="_Toc104104011"></a><a name="_Toc100567714">C.2.3
Recipient to Initiator Messages</a></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Messages send from
recipient to initiator have the following layout for the security header:</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>1.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A </span><span
style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsu:Timestamp</span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'> element if [Timestamp] is 'true'.</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>2.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If the </span><span
style='font-family:"Courier New"'>sp:IncludeToken</span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'> attribute on the [Encryption Token] is
.../IncludeToken/Always, then the [Encryption Token].</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>3.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If [Derived Keys]
is 'true', then a Derived Key Token, based on the [Encryption Token]. This
Derived Key Token is used for encryption.</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>4.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A reference list
including references to encrypted items. If [Signature Protection] is 'true',
then the reference list MUST include a reference to the message signature from
6 below, and the </span><span style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsse11:SignatureConfirmation</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> elements from 5 below if any. If
[Protection Order] is 'SignBeforeEncrypting', then the reference list MUST
include a reference to all the message parts specified in the EncryptedParts
assertions in the policy. If [Derived Keys] is 'true', then the key in the
token from 2 above MUST be used, otherwise the key in the [Encryption Token]
from 2 above.</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>5.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If [Signature
Confirmation] is 'true' then a </span><span style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsse11:SignatureConfirmation</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> element for each signature in the
corresponding message sent from initiator to recipient. If there are no
signatures in the corresponding message from the initiator to the recipient,
then a </span><span style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsse11:SignatureConfirmation</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> element with no Value attribute.</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>6.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If the [Signature
Token] is not the same as the [Encryption Token], and the </span><span
style='font-family:"Courier New"'>sp:IncludeToken</span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'> attribute on the [Signature Token] is
.../IncludeToken/Always, then the [Signature Token].</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>7.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If [Derived Keys]
is 'true', then a Derived Key Token, based on the [Signature Token]. This Derived
Key Token is used for signature.</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>8.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A signature over
the wsu:Timestamp from 1 above, any </span><span style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsse11:SignatureConfirmation</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> elements from 5 above, and all the
message parts specified in SignedParts assertions in the policy. If [Token
Protection] is 'true', the signature MUST also cover the [Signature Token]
regardless of whether it is included in the message. If [Derived Keys] is
'true', the key in the token from 6 above MUST be used, otherwise the key in
the [Signature Token].</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>9.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If [Protection
Order] is 'EncryptBeforeSigning' then a reference list referencing all the
message parts specified in EncryptedParts assertions in the policy. If [Derived
Keys] is 'true', then the key in the Derived Key Token from 3 above MUST be
used, otherwise the key in the [Encryption Token].</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
diagram illustrates the security header layout for the recipient to initiator
message:</span></p>

<p class=Figure><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><img border=0
width=603 height=488
src="ws-securitypolicy-1.2-errata01-os-complete_files/image017.gif"></span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The arrows on the
right indicate parts that were signed as part of the message signature labeled
Sig<sub>1</sub>. The arrows on the left from boxes labeled Ref<sub>1</sub>
indicate references to parts encrypted using a key based on the [SharedSecret
Token] (not shown in these diagrams as it is referenced as an external token).
Two </span><span style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsse11:SignatureConfirmation</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> elements labeled SC<sub>1</sub> and
SC<sub>2</sub> corresponding to the two signatures in the initial message
illustrated previously is included. In general, the ordering of the items in
the security header follows the most optimal layout for a receiver to process
its contents. The rules used to determine this ordering are described in
Appendix C.</span></p>

<p class=Text><i><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Example:</span></i></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Recipient to
initiator message using EncryptBeforeSigning:</span></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:
solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in;
background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;S:Envelope&gt;<br>
  &lt;S:Header&gt;<br>
    &lt;x:Header1 wsu:Id=&quot;Header1&quot; &gt;<br>
    ...<br>
    &lt;/x:Header1&gt;<br>
    &lt;wsse11:EncryptedHeader wsu:Id=&quot;enc_Header2&quot;&gt;<br>
      &lt;!-- Plaintext Header2<br>
      &lt;x:Header2 wsu:Id=&quot;Header2&quot; &gt;<br>
      ...<br>
      &lt;/x:Header2&gt;<br>
      --&gt;<br>
      ...<br>
    &lt;/wsse11:EncryptedHeader&gt;<br>
    ...<br>
    &lt;wsse:Security&gt;<br>
      &lt;wsu:Timestamp wsu:Id=&quot;Timestamp&quot;&gt;<br>
        &lt;wsu:Created&gt;...&lt;/wsu:Created&gt;<br>
        &lt;wsu:Expires&gt;...&lt;/wsu:Expires&gt;<br>
      &lt;/wsu:Timestamp&gt;<br>
      &lt;xenc:ReferenceList&gt;<br>
        &lt;xenc:DataReference URI=&quot;#enc_Signature&quot; /&gt;<br>
        &lt;xenc:DataReference URI=&quot;#enc_SigConf1&quot; /&gt;<br>
        &lt;xenc:DataReference URI=&quot;#enc_SigConf2&quot; /&gt;<br>
        ...<br>
      &lt;/xenc:ReferenceList&gt;<br>
      &lt;xenc:EncryptedData ID=&quot;enc_SigConf1&quot; &gt;<br>
        &lt;!-- Plaintext SignatureConfirmation<br>
        &lt;wsse11:SignatureConfirmation wsu:Id=&quot;SigConf1&quot; &gt;<br>
        ...<br>
        &lt;/wsse11:SignatureConfirmation&gt;<br>
        --&gt;<br>
      ...<br>
      &lt;/xenc:EncryptedData&gt;<br>
      &lt;xenc:EncryptedData ID=&quot;enc_SigConf2&quot; &gt;<br>
        &lt;!-- Plaintext SignatureConfirmation<br>
        &lt;wsse11:SignatureConfirmation wsu:Id=&quot;SigConf2&quot; &gt;<br>
        ...<br>
        &lt;/wsse11:SignatureConfirmation&gt;<br>
        --&gt;<br>
      ...<br>
      &lt;/xenc:EncryptedData&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><br>
      &lt;xenc:EncryptedData Id=&quot;enc_Signature&quot;&gt;<br>
        &lt;!-- Plaintext Signature<br>
        &lt;ds:Signature Id=&quot;Signature&quot;&gt;<br>
          &lt;ds:SignedInfo&gt;<br>
            &lt;ds:References&gt;<br>
              &lt;ds:Reference URI=&quot;#Timestamp&quot;
&gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br>
              &lt;ds:Reference URI=&quot;#SigConf1&quot;
&gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br>
              &lt;ds:Reference URI=&quot;#SigConf2&quot;
&gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br>
              &lt;ds:Reference URI=&quot;#Header1&quot;
&gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br>
              &lt;ds:Reference URI=&quot;#Header2&quot;
&gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br>
              &lt;ds:Reference URI=&quot;#Body&quot;
&gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br>
            &lt;/ds:References&gt;<br>
          &lt;/ds:SignedInfo&gt;<br>
          &lt;ds:SignatureValue&gt;...&lt;/ds:SignatureValue&gt;<br>
          &lt;ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br>
            &lt;wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br>
              &lt;wsse:Reference URI=&quot;#_SomeIssuedToken&quot; /&gt;<br>
            &lt;/wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br>
          &lt;/ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br>
        &lt;/ds:Signature&gt;<br>
        --&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><span
style='color:blue'>        </span>&lt;/xenc:EncryptedData&gt;<br>
        ...<br>
        &lt;ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br>
          &lt;wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br>
            &lt;wsse:Reference URI=&quot;#_SomeIssuedToken&quot; /&gt;<br>
          &lt;/wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br>
        &lt;/ds:KeyInfo&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>     
&lt;xenc:EncryptedData&gt;<br>
      &lt;xenc:ReferenceList&gt;<br>
        &lt;xenc:DataReference URI=&quot;#enc_Body&quot; /&gt;<br>
        &lt;xenc:DataReference URI=&quot;#enc_Header2&quot; /&gt;<br>
        ...<br>
      &lt;/xenc:ReferenceList&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><span
style='color:blue'>   </span>&lt;/xenc:EncryptedData&gt;<br>
    &lt;/wsse:Security&gt;<br>
  &lt;/S:Header&gt;  </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  &lt;S:Body
wsu:Id=&quot;Body&quot;&gt;<br>
    &lt;xenc:EncryptedData Id=&quot;enc_Body&quot;&gt;<br>
      ...<br>
      &lt;ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br>
        &lt;wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br>
          &lt;wsse:Reference URI=&quot;#_SomeIssuedToken&quot; /&gt;<br>
        &lt;/wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br>
      &lt;/ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br>
    &lt;/xenc:EncryptedData&gt;<br>
  &lt;/S:Body&gt;<br>
&lt;/S:Envelope&gt;</p>

</div>

<p class=AppendixHeading2><a name="_Toc325572805"></a><a name="_Toc182291584"></a><a
name="_Toc161826608"></a><a name="_Toc104104012"></a><a name="_Toc100567715">C.3
Asymmetric Binding</a></p>

<p class=Text><a name="_Toc99269313"></a><a name="_Toc96244178"></a><a
name="_Toc95877124"></a><a name="_Toc95113129"></a><a name="_Toc85362827"></a><a
name="_Toc85350272"></a><a name="_Toc79810745"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
section describes how the ‘Strict’ security header layout rules apply to the
Asymmetric Binding.</span></a></p>

<p class=AppendixHeading3><a name="_Toc325572806"></a><a name="_Toc182291585"></a><a
name="_Toc161826609"></a><a name="_Toc104104013"></a><a name="_Toc100567716">C.3.1
Policy</a></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
example shows a policy indicating an Asymmetric Binding, an X509 token as the
[Initiator Token], an X509 token as the [Recipient Token]<a name="_Toc85362830"></a><a
name="_Toc85350275"></a><a name="_Toc79810748">, an algorithm suite, a
requirement to encrypt the message parts before signing, a requirement to
encrypt the message signature, a requirement to include tokens in the message
signature and the supporting signatures, a requirement to include </a></span><span
style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsse11:SignatureConfirmation</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> elements, a username token attached
to the message, and finally an X509 token attached to the message and endorsing
the message signature. Minimum message protection requirements are described as
well.</span></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:
solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in;
background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;!--
Example Endpoint Policy --&gt;<br>
&lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp=&quot;...&quot; xmlns:sp=&quot;...&quot;&gt;<br>
  &lt;sp:AsymmetricBinding&gt;<br>
    &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
      &lt;sp:RecipientToken&gt;<br>
        &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
          &lt;sp:X509Token sp:IncludeToken=&quot;.../IncludeToken/Always&quot;
/&gt;<br>
        &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
     &lt;/sp:RecipientToken&gt;<br>
     &lt;sp:InitiatorToken&gt;<br>
       &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
         &lt;sp:X509Token sp:IncludeToken=&quot;.../IncludeToken/Always&quot;
/&gt;<br>
       &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
     &lt;/sp:InitiatorToken&gt;<br>
     &lt;sp:AlgorithmSuite&gt;<br>
       &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
         &lt;sp:Basic256 /&gt;<br>
       &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
     &lt;/sp:AlgorithmSuite&gt;<br>
     &lt;sp:Layout&gt;<br>
        &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
          &lt;sp:Strict /&gt;<br>
        &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
     &lt;/sp:Layout&gt;<br>
     &lt;sp:IncludeTimestamp /&gt;<br>
     &lt;sp:EncryptBeforeSigning /&gt;<br>
     &lt;sp:EncryptSignature /&gt;<br>
     &lt;sp:ProtectTokens /&gt;<br>
   &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
  &lt;/sp:AsymmetricBinding&gt;<br>
  &lt;sp:SignedEncryptedSupportingTokens&gt;<br>
    &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
      &lt;sp:UsernameToken sp:IncludeToken=&quot;.../IncludeToken/Once&quot;
/&gt;<br>
    &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
  &lt;/sp:SignedEncryptedSupportingTokens&gt;<br>
  &lt;sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens&gt;<br>
    &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
      &lt;sp:X509Token sp:IncludeToken=&quot;.../IncludeToken/Once&quot;&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>       
&lt;wsp:Policy&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>         
&lt;sp:WssX509v3Token10 /&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>        &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;</p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>     
&lt;/sp:X509Token&gt;<br>
    &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
  &lt;/sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens&gt;<br>
  &lt;sp:Wss11&gt;<br>
    &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
      &lt;sp:RequireSignatureConfirmation /&gt;<br>
    &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
  &lt;/sp:Wss11&gt;<br>
&lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br>
<br>
</p>

</div>

<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:
solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in;
background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;!--
Example Message Policy --&gt;<br>
&lt;wsp:All xmlns:wsp=&quot;...&quot; xmlns:sp=&quot;...&quot;&gt;<br>
  &lt;sp:SignedParts&gt;<br>
    &lt;sp:Header Name=&quot;Header1&quot; Namespace=&quot;...&quot; /&gt;<br>
    &lt;sp:Header Name=&quot;Header2&quot; Namespace=&quot;...&quot; /&gt;<br>
    &lt;sp:Body/&gt;<br>
  &lt;/sp:SignedParts&gt;<br>
  &lt;sp:EncryptedParts&gt;<br>
    &lt;sp:Header Name=&quot;Header2&quot; Namespace=&quot;...&quot; /&gt;<br>
    &lt;sp:Body/&gt;<br>
  &lt;/sp:EncryptedParts&gt;<br>
&lt;/wsp:All&gt;</p>

</div>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This policy is
used as the basis for the examples shown in the subsequent section describing
the security header layout for this binding.</span></p>

<p class=AppendixHeading3><a name="_Toc325572807"></a><a name="_Toc182291586"></a><a
name="_Toc161826610"></a><a name="_Toc104104014"></a><a name="_Toc100567717">C.3.2
Initiator to Recipient Messages</a></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Messages sent from
initiator to recipient have the following layout:</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>1.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A </span><span
style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsu:Timestamp</span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'> element if [Timestamp] is 'true'.</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>2.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If a [Recipient
Token] is specified, and the associated </span><span style='font-family:"Courier New"'>sp:IncludeToken</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> attribute is .../IncludeToken/Once or
.../IncludeToken/Always, then the [Recipient Token]. </span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>3.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If a [Recipient
Token] is specified and [Protection Order] is 'SignBeforeEncrypting' or
[SignatureProtection] is 'true' then an xenc:EncryptedKey element, containing a
key encrypted for the recipient. The xenc:EncryptedKey element MUST include an
xenc:ReferenceList containing a reference to all the message parts specified in
EncryptedParts assertions in the policy. If [Signature Protection] is 'true'
then the reference list MUST contain a reference to the message signature from
6 below. It is an error if [Signature Protection] is 'true' and there is not a
message signature.</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>4.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Any tokens from
the supporting tokens properties (as defined in section 8) whose </span><span
style='font-family:"Courier New"'>sp:IncludeToken </span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>attribute is .../IncludeToken/Once or
.../IncludeToken/Always.</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>5.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If an [Initiator
Token] is specified, and the associated </span><span style='font-family:"Courier New"'>sp:IncludeToken</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> attribute is .../IncludeToken/Once or
.../IncludeToken/Always, then the [Initiator Token]. </span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>6.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A signature based
on the key in the [Initiator Token] if specified, over the </span><span
style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsu:Timestamp</span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'> from 1 above, any tokens from 4 above regardless of
whether they are included in the message, and any message parts specified in
SignedParts assertions in the policy. If [Token Protection] is 'true', the
signature MUST also cover the [Initiator Token] regardless of whether it is
included in the message.</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>7.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Signatures for
tokens from the [Endorsing Supporting Tokens] and [Signed Endorsing Supporting
Tokens] properties. If [Derived Keys] is 'true' and the supporting token is
associated with a symmetric key, then a Derived Key Token, based on the
supporting token, appears before the signature. If [Token Protection] is
'true', the signature MUST also cover the supporting token regardless of
whether it is included in the message.</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>8.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If a [Recipient
Token] is specified and [Protection Order] is 'EncryptBeforeSigning' then if
[Signature Protection] is 'false' then an xenc:EncryptedKey element, containing
a key encrypted for the recipient and a reference list, else if [Signature
Protection] is 'true', a reference list. The reference list includes a reference
to all the message parts specified in EncryptedParts assertions in the policy.
The encrypted parts MUST reference the key contained in the xenc:EncryptedKey
element from 3 above.</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
diagram illustrates the security header layout for the initiator to recipient
messages:</span></p>

<p class=Figure><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><img border=0
width=591 height=570
src="ws-securitypolicy-1.2-errata01-os-complete_files/image018.gif"></span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The arrows on the
right indicate parts that were signed as part of the message signature labeled
Sig<sub>2</sub> using the [Initiator Token] labeled ST<sub>2</sub>. The dashed
arrows on the left from the box labeled Sig<sub>3</sub> indicate the parts
signed by the supporting token ST<sub>3</sub>, namely the message signature Sig<sub>2</sub>
and the token used as the basis for the signature labeled ST<sub>3</sub>. The
arrows on the left from boxes labeled EK<sub>1</sub> indicate references to
parts encrypted using a key encrypted for the [Recipient Token] labeled ST<sub>1</sub>.
The arrows on the left from boxes labeled Ref<sub>1</sub> indicate additional
references to parts encrypted using the key contained in the encrypted key
labeled EK<sub>1</sub>. The dotted arrows inside the box labeled Security
indicate the token used as the basis for each cryptographic operation. In
general, the ordering of the items in the security header follows the most
optimal layout for a receiver to process its contents. The rules used to
determine this ordering are described in Appendix C.</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Note: In most
typical scenarios, the recipient key is not included in the message, but rather
the encrypted key contains an external reference to the token containing the
encryption key. The diagram illustrates how one might attach a security token
related to the encrypted key for completeness. One possible use-case for this
approach might be a stack which does not support the STR Dereferencing
Transform, but wishes to include the encryption token in the message signature.</span></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Initiator to
recipient message <i>Example</i></span></p>

<div style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom:
solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in;
background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>&lt;S:Envelope 
xmlns:S=&quot;...&quot; xmlns:x=&quot;...&quot; xmlns:wsu=&quot;...&quot; </p>

<p class=Code style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>  
xmlns:wsse11=&quot;...&quot; xmlns:wsse=&quot;...&quot;
xmlns:xenc=&quot;...&quot; xmlns:ds=&quot;...&quot;&gt;<br>
  &lt;S:Header&gt;<br>
    &lt;x:Header1 wsu:Id=&quot;Header1&quot; &gt;<br>
    ...<br>
    &lt;/x:Header1&gt;<br>
    &lt;wsse11:EncryptedHeader wsu:Id=&quot;enc_Header2&quot;&gt;<br>
      &lt;!-- Plaintext Header2<br>
      &lt;x:Header2 wsu:Id=&quot;Header2&quot; &gt;<br>
      ...<br>
      &lt;/x:Header2&gt;<br>
      --&gt;<br>
      ...<br>
    &lt;/wsse11:EncryptedHeader&gt;<br>
    ...<br>
    &lt;wsse:Security&gt;<br>
      &lt;wsu:Timestamp wsu:Id=&quot;Timestamp&quot;&gt;<br>
        &lt;wsu:Created&gt;...&lt;/wsu:Created&gt;<br>
        &lt;wsu:Expires&gt;...&lt;/wsu:Expires&gt;<br>
      &lt;/wsu:Timestamp&gt;<br>
      &lt;wsse:BinarySecurityToken wsu:Id=&quot;RecipientToken&quot; &gt;<br>
      ...<br>
      &lt;/wsse:BinarySecurityToken&gt;<br>
      &lt;xenc:EncryptedKey wsu:Id=&quot;RecipientEncryptedKey&quot; &gt;<br>
        ...<br>
        &lt;xenc:ReferenceList&gt;<br>
          &lt;xenc:DataReference URI=&quot;#enc_Signature&quot; /&gt;<br>
          &lt;xenc:DataReference URI=&quot;#enc_SomeUsernameToken&quot; /&gt;<br>
          ...<br>
        &lt;/xenc:ReferenceList&gt;<br>
      &lt;/xenc:EncryptedKey&gt;<br>
      &lt;xenc:EncryptedData ID=&quot;enc_SomeUsernameToken&quot; &gt;<br>
        &lt;!-- Plaintext UsernameToken<br>
        &lt;wsse:UsernameToken wsu:Id=&quot;SomeUsernameToken&quot; &gt;<br>
        ...<br>
        &lt;/wsse:UsernameToken&gt; <br>
        --&gt;<br>
        ...<br>
      &lt;/xenc:EncryptedData&gt;<br>
      &lt;wsse:BinarySecurityToken wsu:Id=&quot;SomeSupportingToken&quot; &gt;<br>
      ...<br>
      &lt;/wsse:BinarySecurityToken&gt;<br>
      &lt;wsse:BinarySecurityToken wsu:Id=&quot;InitiatorToken&quot; &gt;<br>
      ...<br>
      &lt;/wsse:BinarySecurityToken&gt;<br>
      &lt;xenc:EncryptedData ID=&quot;enc_Signature&quot;&gt;<br>
        &lt;!-- Plaintext Signature<br>
        &lt;ds:Signature Id=&quot;Signature&quot;&gt;<br>
          &lt;ds:SignedInfo&gt;<br>
            &lt;ds:References&gt;<br>
              &lt;ds:Reference URI=&quot;#Timestamp&quot;
&gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br>
              &lt;ds:Reference URI=&quot;#SomeUsernameToken&quot;
&gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br>
              &lt;ds:Reference URI=&quot;#SomeSupportingToken&quot; &gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br>
              &lt;ds:Reference URI=&quot;#InitiatorToken&quot;
&gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br>
              &lt;ds:Reference URI=&quot;#Header1&quot;
&gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br>
              &lt;ds:Reference URI=&quot;#Header2&quot;
&gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br>
              &lt;ds:Reference URI=&quot;#Body&quot; &gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br>
            &lt;/ds:References&gt;<br>
          &lt;/ds:SignedInfo&gt;<br>
          &lt;ds:SignatureValue&gt;...&lt;/ds:SignatureValue&gt;<br>
          &lt;ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br>
            &lt;wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br>
              &lt;wsse:Reference URI=&quot;#InitiatorToken&quot; /&gt;<br>
            &lt;/wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br>
          &lt;/ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br>
        &lt;/ds:Signature&gt;<br>
        --&gt;<br>
        ...<br>
      &lt;/xenc:EncryptedData&gt;<br>
      &lt;ds:Signature&gt;<br>
        &lt;ds:SignedInfo&gt;<br>
          &lt;ds:References&gt;<br>
            &lt;ds:Reference URI=&quot;#Signature&quot; &gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br>
            &lt;ds:Reference URI=&quot;#SomeSupportingToken&quot;
&gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br>
          &lt;/ds:References&gt;<br>
        &lt;/ds:SignedInfo&gt;<br>
        &lt;ds:SignatureValue&gt;...&lt;/ds:SignatureValue&gt;<br>
        &lt;ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br>
          &lt;wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br>
            &lt;wsse:Reference URI=&quot;#SomeSupportingToken&quot; /&gt;<br>
          &lt;/wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br>
        &lt;/ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br>
      &lt;/ds:Signature&gt;<br>
      &lt;xenc:ReferenceList&gt;<br>
        &lt;xenc:DataReference URI=&quot;#enc_Body&quot; /&gt;<br>
        &lt;xenc:DataReference URI=&quot;#enc_Header2&quot; /&gt;<br>
        ...<br>
      &lt;/xenc:ReferenceList&gt;<br>
    &lt;/wsse:Security&gt;<br>
  &lt;/S:Header&gt;<br>
  &lt;S:Body wsu:Id=&quot;Body&quot;&gt;<br>
    &lt;xenc:EncryptedData Id=&quot;enc_Body&quot;&gt;<br>
      ...<br>
      &lt;ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br>
        &lt;wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br>
          &lt;wsse:Reference URI=&quot;#RecipientEncryptedKey&quot; /&gt;<br>
        &lt;/wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br>
      &lt;/ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br>
    &lt;/xenc:EncryptedData&gt;<br>
  &lt;/S:Body&gt;<br>
&lt;/S:Envelope&gt;</p>

</div>

<p class=AppendixHeading3><a name="_Toc325572808"></a><a name="_Toc182291587"></a><a
name="_Toc161826611"></a><a name="_Toc104104015"></a><a name="_Toc100567718">C.3.3
Recipient to Initiator Messages</a></p>

<p class=Text><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Messages sent from
recipient to initiator have the following layout:</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>1.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A </span><span
style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsu:Timestamp</span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'> element if [Timestamp] is 'true'.</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>2.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If an [Initiator
Token] is specified, and the associated </span><span style='font-family:"Courier New"'>sp:IncludeToken</span><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'> attribute is .../IncludeToken/Always,
then the [Initiator Token].</span></p>

<p class=Text style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>3.<span style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
</span></span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If an [Initiator
Token] is specified and [Protection Order] is 'SignBeforeEncrypting' or
[SignatureProtection] is 'true' then an xenc:EncryptedKey element, containing a
key encrypted for the initiator. The xenc:EncryptedKey element MUST include an
xenc:ReferenceList containing a reference to all the message parts specified in
EncryptedParts assertions in the policy. If [Signature Protection] is 'true'
then the reference list MUST also contain a reference to the message signature
from 6 below, if any and references to the </span><span style='font-family:
"Courier New"'>wsse11:SignatureConfirmation</span><span style='font-family:
"Arial","sans-serif"'> elements from 4 below, if any.</span></p>
